patippuri
ilakkiyilakkaṇaccemmalmutumuṉivarayyāiḷaṅkumaraṉāravarkaḷeḻupattintuāṇṭuniṟivueytiyatippōṟṟumvakiyiltamiḻiḷiyarkkeṉṟuavareḻutiyanūlkaḷiyellāmcērttueḻupattintunūlkaḷkaṭanta2005-āmāṇṭutiruccittirunakarilveḷiyiṭṭuempatippuppaṇikkupperumicērttukkoṇṭōm.
antavakiyil2010-āmāṇṭuipperun
tamiḻācāṉ81-āmāṇṭuniṟivikkoṇṭāṭum
vakiyiltamiḻmoḻi-iṉa-nāṭṭiṉkāppiṟkākavum,mīṭpiṟkākavum,mēṉmikkākavumivareḻutiyaaṉittuaṟivuccelvaṅkaḷiyumtokuttupporuḷvaḻippirittu20tokutikaḷākaveḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm.maturittamiḻccaṅkaccentamiḻitaḻukkum,civacittāntanūṟpatippukkaḻakamnaṭattivarumcentamiḻccelvi,kuṟaḷiyammaṟṟumpiṟitaḻkaḷukkum,malarkaḷukkumivareḻutiyaaṟiviṉākkaṅkaḷittokuttuttamiḻkūṟumnallulakiṟkuvaḻaṅkumpaṇiyilumīṭupaṭṭuḷḷōm.
ipperumakaṉvāḻumkālattilēyēivareḻutiyeḻuttukkaḷ,pēciyapēccukkaḷaṉittiyumtokuttuveḷiyiṭuvatueṉpatutamiḻnūlpatippuvaralāṟṟilpōṟṟimakiḻvataṟkāṉa,aṉivarumpiṉpaṟṟuvataṟkāṉaarumperumtamiḻppaṇiyākum.
ivartannalamkarutāmaltamiḻnalamkāttuvarupavar.tammimuṉṉiṟuttātutamiḻimuṉṉiṟuttumperuntamiḻaṟiñar.tamakkeṉavāḻātutamiḻkkeṉavāḻpavar.orunāḷiṉmuḻuppoḻutumtamiḻākavēvāḻumtamiḻppōrāḷi.caṅkaccāṉṟōrvariciyilvittueṇṇattakkaaruntamiḻaṟiñar.tamiḻilakkaṇa-ilakkiyamarapikkāttuvarummarapuvaḻiaṟiñar.āravārammikuntiṉṟiyacūḻniliyilpaṭāṭōpamiṉṟiyum,viḷamparappōlimiiṉṟiyum,tamiḻmoḻiyiṉāḻaakalaṅkaḷiakaḻntukāṭṭumtoltamiḻaṟiñar.moḻinūlkatiravaṉpāvāṇariṉvērccolāyvilavarkāṭṭiyavaḻiyiltamtamiḻāyvittoṭarpavar.
ivareḻutikkuvittatamiḻaṟivuccelvaṅkaḷiavariṭamēvēṇṭippeṟṟu20tokutikaḷākiḷaṅkumaraṉārtamiḻvaḷameṉumtalippilporuḷvaḻippirittuveḷiyiṭukiṟōm.tamiḻāyvukkaḷattiltamāyvuppayaṇattittoṭaṅkumtamiḻāyvāḷarkaḷukkum,tamiḻuṇarvāḷarkaḷukkum,māṇavaccelvaṅkaḷukkumtaṅkattaṭṭilvittuppoṟkuviyalākattantuḷḷōm.emtamiḻnūlpatippuppayaṇattil,tamiḻtteṉṟaltiru.vi.ka.viyum,taṉittamiḻiyakkattantimaṟimaliyaṭikaḷiyum,moḻinūlkatiravaṉpāvāṇariyum,tamiḻkmoḻikkāvalarilakkuvaṉāriyumvaḻikāṭṭiyākakkoṇṭutamiḻnūlparappiṉelliyikkaṇṭukāṭṭumcaṅkattamiḻtcāṉṟōrākaviḷaṅkumiyāiḷaṅkumaraṉārvāḻumkālattilēyēavarnūlkaḷiveḷiyiṭuvatiyāmpeṟṟapēṟākakkarutukiṟōm.tamiḻarillamtōṟumirukkattakkivvaruntamiḻccelvaṅkaḷittamiḻiḷamtalimuṟikkuvippākakkoṭuttuḷḷōm.
oṇṭamiḻttāycilampaṭiyiṉ

ilakkaṇaakarāti
(aintilakkaṇam)
eḻuttu,col,poruḷ,yāppuaṇi








aintilakkaṇaccurukkam
tamiḻilakkaṇam,eḻuttucolporuḷeṉattolkāppiyattilnaṭiyiṭṭatu.
eḻuttumcollumanniliyilēniṉṟaṉa.
poruḷmukkūṟupaṭuvaṉāyiṟṟu	.
tolkāppiyarkūṟumakattiṇiiyal,kaḷaviyal,kaṟpiyal,poruḷiyaleṉpaviiṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyal,nampiyakapporuḷ,māṟaṉakapporuḷeṉaakanūlkaḷāyiṉa.
puṟattiṇiiyalakattiṇinūlkaḷpōlvirivuṟāmalpuṟapporuḷveṇpāmālieṉṟuamintatu.ataṉpiṉṉarppuṟappāṭalkaḷittiraṭṭiyapuṟattiraṭṭueṉṟuorutokinūluruvākiyatu.aintilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyumkūṟumnūlkaḷilōraḷavālcollappaṭṭuamintuniṉṟatu.
yāppunūl,kākkipāṭiṉiyamciṟukākkipāṭiṉiyamavinayamnaṟṟattameṉappalappalanūlkaḷāykkiḷarntaṉa.
eṉiṉumyāpparuṅkalam,yāpparuṅkalakkārikieṉpavitōṉṟiyapiṉ-avaṟṟukkuariyaperiyuriviḷakkamvāyttapiṉ-paḻiyayāppunūlkaḷiyellāmavvurikaḷiṉvaḻiyākaaṟiyumāṟuamintaṉa.tolkāppiyacceyyuḷiyalkoṇṭukiḷarntaṉavēyāppunūlkaḷām.
iṉittolkāppiyuvamiiyalkoṇṭukiḷarntaṉataṇṭiyalaṅkāram,māṟaṉalaṅkārameṉpavi.viriyaviriyaaṇikaḷipperukkiṉa;ataṉioṭṭivaṭamoḻivaḻiyēaṇiilakkaṇapperukkamēṟpaṭṭu,uvamieṉṉumtāyaṇiyiṉiyaṟkieḻilcitiyalāyiṟṟu.collaṇieṉṉumnayamikkaaṇi,moḻinalaṉukkuettakukēṭuceyyamuṭiyumōavvaḷavumceyyaaṇinūlkaḷiṭamtantaṉa.
pāṭṭiyalnūlkaḷeḻuttupāmutaliyavaṟṟukkumvaruṇamkāṭṭiyumnacceḻuttu,amuteḻuttu,poruttamkaṇiyameṉiyaṟkikkumāṟākanaṭiyiṭṭuccilanūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ(18,19)ilakkiyattiṉiyaṟkivaḷam-poruḷnayam-keṭuttaṉa.
innilikkuppiṉiyaṟkiyoṭutaḻuviyeḷiyanaṭi,iṭarilāvakiyilporuḷaṟivāyppueṉpaviamiyappāṭṭiyaṟṟumnilipaṭippaṭiyēamintatu.urinaṭiyākkamumuruppeṟṟatu.
pāṭaloṭu,urippā,urivīccu,putuppā,aikkū,tuḷippāeṉappallapalakiḷarntaṉa.
ivvakiyilākkamumkēṭumāmvakiyiltamiḻilakkaṇilakkiyaṅkaḷvaḷarntukoṇṭuḷa.
ellāvakippāvakikkumiṭamtara,ellāvakipputumikkumvaḷarccikkumiṭamtaraamintanūltol
kāppiyam.ataṉvarampu-moḻikkāvalvarampu-oṉṟēoṉṟu.moḻikkēṭuākāvakiyilmoḻiyippayaṉ
paṭuttukeṉpatē!
uḷḷatucitippōruḷareṉappaṭāareṉpatucāṉṟōrāṇi!
virivupaṭṭilakkaṇaṅkaḷilōraḷavālcilavaṟṟittokuttueḻuttu,col,akapporuḷ,puṟapporuḷ,yāppu,aṇieṉṉummuṟiyilakarappaṭuttivēṇṭumaḷavālporuḷumviḷakkamumeṭuttukkāṭṭumtarappaṭṭanūliḵtākaliṉintilakkaṇaccurukkameṉappaṭṭatām!
itaṉeḻuttumcollummuṉṉarēcivacittāntanūṟpatippukkaḻakavaḻiyēveḷippaṭṭatu.itukāl,aintilak
kaṇaṅkaḷumorumottamākavaḷavaṉpatippakavaḻiyēveḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyurikaḷ,akarātikaḷ,ilakkaṇappērakarātieṉakkaruvinūlkaḷittokutitokutiyākaveḷiyiṭṭatamiḻmaṇpatippakam,iccurukkaakarātiyiyumveḷiyiṭukiṉṟatu.ivveḷiyīṭṭuppoṟuppimēṟkoṇṭatamiḻttoṇṭar,tamiḻppōrāḷi,tamiḻkkoṭiñarkō.iḷavaḻakaṉārkkuppatippāḷariṉiyarkkumnaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
innūltokuppiuḷaṅkoṇṭutiraṭṭiyapulamiyarkaruppakkiḷarpulavarcu.a.irāmacāmi,pulavarvē.vaṭukanātaṉeṉpār.attokiyivēṇṭumaḷavāltokuttumvirittumeṭuttukkāṭṭuttantumviḷakkiyaaḷavēempaṇiyām.avarkaṭkuemnaṉṟiyaṟitaluṭiyatu.
aṇiyilakkaṇappakutiyiṉvirivuāyvuceyvārāyvukkuiṭamākutalkarutiyateṉaamitikūṟutalcālum!
	tamiḻuvamittāytaṉippēreḻilum,
	ataṉicnurtaṇṇoḷikkatirvīccum
	ettaṉiettaṉimayakkappaḷiccukaḷukku
	iṭamtantuāriyavaḻippaṭṭaṉeṉpati
	muṟṟilumiṉṟēṉumoraḷavēṉumaṟintu
	koṇṭuiyaṟkieḻilaṇiciṟakkutavumeṉa
	vakipaleṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷa
	vilakkalāmēeṉiṉ,kēṭṭiyum
	vilakkalāmēeṉiṉ,kēṭṭiyum
	viḷaṅkivilakalāmēeṉpatuuḷḷīṭām
kālantōṟumvaḷarummoḻiilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷ,tokiyākkappaṭuttutalkaṭṭāyattēvi.ataṉic,cemmoḻiamippumēṟkoṇṭutakkavarkaḷkūṭṭuḻippāluruvākkappaṭiṉ,etirkālamoḻinalappāṭṭukkuariyakaruviyākaamiyumeṉpatuuṟutiyām.
vāḻiyanalaṉē!vāḻiyanilaṉē!
iṉpaaṉpuṭaṉ
irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ



1.eḻuttu
a
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉneṭuṅkaṇakkilvarummutaleḻuttu.uyirmuyaṟciyāṟpiṟakkumeḻuttukkaḷuḷvāyittiṟantaaḷavilēolikkuṅkuṟṟeḻuttu.aḵṟiṇippaṉmippeyarīṟu.aḵṟiṇippaṉmiviṉimuṟṟuvikuti.āṟāvataṉpaṉmiyurupu;peyareccaviṉiyeccaviyaṅkōḷvikuti.iṉmi,etirmaṟi,kuṟivu,uṭaṉpāṭu,taṭi,piṟitu,viyappuivaṟṟikkāṭṭumorutuṇivaravu(upacarkkam)eṭṭeṉṉuntamiḻeṇṇākavaruvatumivveḻuttē.paṇṭaṟicuṭṭuakaccuṭṭukaḷukkumuṉṉumvarum.eṭuttukkāṭṭu:akkālam;antamaṉitaṉ,antakkōyil.ivveḻuttuakaram;akāram,aḵkāṉeṉackaraccāriyiyumkāraccāriyiyumkāṉcāriyiyumpeṟṟuvaḻaṅkum.
akaccuṭṭu-a,i,ueṉṉumeḻuttukaḷcoṟkaḷukkuuḷḷēyēniṉṟuporuḷiccuṭṭikkāṭṭumāyiṉappoḻutuavveḻuttukaḷakaccuṭṭueṉṟukūṟappeṟum.
(e.ṭu)avaṉ,ivaṉ,uvaṉ.
akalavuri:
viritturi.aḵtuilakkaṇamumilakkiyamumeṭuttukkāṭṭipporuḷviritturikkuñciṟappuri.
akaviṉā:
coṟkaḷukkuḷḷēyēyiruntuviṉāpporuḷtarumcoṟkaḷukkuakaviṉāeṉṟupeyar.(e.kā)evaṉ,yātu,ētu?
akameṉpataṟkucciṟappuviti:
akameṉṉumuḷḷiṭappeyarmuṉcevi,kieṉṉumciṉippeyarkaḷvantālnilimoḻiyiṟutimakaramvaṉmikkuiṉamākattiritalēyaṉṟiataṉnaṭuvilniṉṟakakarameyyumataṉmēlēṟiyaakaravuyiruṅkeṭum.
	(e.ṭu)	akam+cevi=añceviañceviniṟiya
		akam+ki=aṅkiaṅkiyuḷnelli
akammuṉvarumkaṇ,taṇ,ciṟieṉpaṉavumavvāṟuvarum.
	akam+kaṇ=akaṅkaṇ;aṅkaṇar=civaperumāṉ.
	akam+taṇ=antaṇ;antaṇar=aruḷāḷar.
	akam+ciṟi=añciṟi;añciṟittumpi
	akammuṉarccevikivariṉiṭiyaṉakeṭum.
-naṉ222.
akaṉ:
akaṉṟeṉṉumporuḷilvarumpeyareccam.(e.ṭu)akaṉmaṉi,akaṉnilam.
akkuccāriyiyiṉīṟutirital:
evvakippaṭṭapeyarmuṉṉumvalleḻuttuvarumiṭattuakkiṉiṟutikkuṟṟiyalukarammuṭiyattōṉṟātu.ataṉāṟpaṟṟappaṭṭavalleḻuttākiyameytaṉakkumēlniṉṟameyyoṭumkeṭum.(e.ṭu)kuṉṟattukkūki>kuṉṟakkūki,maṉṟattuppaṇṇi>maṉṟappaṇṇi.
aciniliōkāram:
ceyyuḷiṉīṟṟilōkāramaciyākaniṟpatu.(e.ṭu)kēṇmiṉō
aciniliyaḷapeṭi:
acikōṭaṟporuṭṭukkoṇṭatōraḷapeṭi.
	(e.ṭu)	ōōiṉitēemakkinnōyceytakaṇ
		tāamitaṟpaṭṭatu.
tiruk.1176
atueṉpataṟkucciṟappuviti:
atueṉṉuñcuṭṭuppeyariṉmuṉvarumoḻiyākavarukiṟaaṉṟueṉṉumetirmaṟikkuṟippuviṉimuṟṟuceyyuḷilāṉṟueṉamutalnīḷum.

ammiṉiṟutitirital:
ammiṉiṟutiyākiyamakarameykacatapakkaḷvarumoḻiyākavantakālattuttaṉvaṭivutirintuṅañanakkaḷākum.

alvaḻippuṇarcci:
vēṟṟumiallātavaḻippuṇarvatualvaḻippuṇarcciyākum.atuviṉittoki,paṇputtoki,uvamittoki,ummittoki,aṉmoḻittokieṉṉumintutokinilittoṭarum,eḻuvāyttoṭar,viḷittoṭar,teriniliviṉimuṟṟuttoṭar,kuṟippuviṉimuṟṟuttoṭar,peyareccattoṭar,viṉiyeccattoṭar,iṭiccoṟṟoṭar,uriccoṟṟoṭar,aṭukkuttoṭareṉṉumoṉpatutokānilittoṭarkaḷumākappatiṉāṉkuvakippaṭum.
(e.ṭu)	tokinilittoṭarkaḷ:
	kolyāṉi	-	viṉittoki
	karuṅkutiri	-	paṇputtoki
	paṉimaram		-	irupeyaroṭṭuppaṇputtoki
	matimukam		-	uvamittoki
	kapilaparaṇar	-	ummittoki
	karuṅkuḻal		-	aṉmoḻittoki
tokānilittoṭar:
vaḷavaṉvantāṉ	-	eḻuvāyttoṭar
murukāvā		-	viḷittoṭar
vantāḷkōti	-	teriniliviṉimuṟṟuttoṭar
poṉṉivaṉ	-	kuṟippuviṉimuṟṟuttoṭar
vantapoṉṉi	-	peyareccattoṭar
vantupōṉāṉ	-	viṉiyeccattoṭar
maṟṟoṉṟu		-	iṭiccoṟṟoṭar
naṉipēti		-	uriccoṟṟoṭar
pāmpupāmpu		-	aṭukkuttoṭar
alvaḻiyilkuṟṟiyalukarattiṉmuṉvalliṉampuṇartal:
vaṉṟoṭaroḻintaintutoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukarattiṉmuṉvarukiṟavalliṉamalvaḻippuṇarcciyiliyalpākum.
	(e.ṭu)	āṟu+tali=āṟutali-neṭiṟṟoṭar
		eḵtu+peritu=eḵtuperitu?-āytattoṭar
		varaku+ciṟitu=varakuciṟitu-uyirttoṭar
		vantu+tantāṉ=vantutantāṉ-meṉṟoṭar
		eytu+poruḷ=eytuporuḷ-iṭittoṭar
alvaḻippuṇarcci:
vēṟṟumiallātavaḻippuṇarvatualvaḻippuṇarcci.
aḻaṉeṉpataṟkucciṟappuviti:
aḻaṉeṉṉumcolliṉṉakaravīṟukeṭavalleḻuttumikkumuṭiyum.
	(e.ṭu)	aḻaṉ+kuṭam=aḻakkuṭam.
aḷapeṭi:
ceyyuḷilōcikuṟintuvarumāyiṉavvōciyiniṟikkumporuṭṭuuyireḻuttummeyyeḻuttumtamakkuriyoliaḷaviṉummikuntolikkum.avvāṟumikuntuolippatuaḷapeṭieṉappaṭum.uyireḻuttuaḷapeṭuppatuuyiraḷa
peṭieṉṟum,oṟṟeḻuttuaḷapeṭuppatuoṟṟaḷapeṭieṉṟumākum.aḷapeṭieṉpatumāttirimikutaleṉṟuporuḷpaṭum.iviyēyaṉṟiiṉṉiciaḷapeṭi,colliciaḷapeṭi,aciniliaḷapeṭieṉṟavēṟuaḷapeṭikaḷumuṇṭu.
aḷavukaṭantuolikkumeḻuttukkaḷ:
icittalum,aḻittalum,paṇṭamāṟṟutalumākiyaviṭattuuyireḻuttukaḷummeyyeḻuttukaḷumtamakkuccolliyaaḷavikkaṭantuolikkum.
aḵkiyi:
kuṟumi+iyal+ikaram=kuṟṟiyalikaram.ivvikaramtaṉakkuriyorumāttiriyiṟkuṟintuarimāttiriyākolikkum.itaṉviḷakkattikkuṟṟiyalikarameṉṟatalippilkāṇka.
aḵkiyu:
kuṟṟiyalukaram.kuṟumi+iyal+ukaram=kuṟṟiyalukaram.ōcikuṟukiolikkumukaramkuṟṟiyalukaramākum.itutaṉakkuriyorumāttiriyiṟkuṟintuarimāttiriyāyolikkum.ituāṟuvakippaṭum.itaṉviḷakkattikkuṟṟiyalukarameṉṟatalippilkāṇka.
aḵkiyai:
aikārakkuṟukkam.aḵkutal=kuṟital.aikāramtaṉakkuriyiraṇṭumāttiriyiṟkuṟintuolikkumiṭattuaikārakkuṟukkameṉappeyarpeṟum.itaṉviḷakkattiaikārakkuṟukkameṉṉuntalippilkāṇka.
aḵkiyau:
aukārakkuṟukkam.aukāramtaṉakkuriyiraṇṭumāttiriyiṟkuṟintuolikkumiṭattuaukārakkuṟukkameṉappeyarpeṟum.itaṉviḷakkattiaukārakkuṟukkameṉṉuntalippilkāṇka.
aḵkiyataṉinili:
āytakkuṟukkam.āytamtaṉakkuriyaarimāttiriyiṟkuṟintukālmāttiriyāyolikkumiṭattuāytakkuṟukkameṉappeyarpeṟum.itaṉviḷakkattiāytakkuṟukkameṉṉuntalippilkāṇka.
aḵkiyamaḵkāṉ:
makarakkuṟukkam.makaramtaṉakkuriyaarimāttiriyiṟkuṟintukālmāttiriyāyolikkumiṭattumakarakkuṟukkameṉappeyarpeṟum.itaṉviḷakkattimakaramkuṟukkameṉṉuntalippilkāṇka.
aḵkēṉam:
uyireḻuttuvariciyiṉiṟutiyilvarumpuḷḷivaṭivāṉeḻuttuaḵkēṉamāytameṉpatumatu(ḵ).

		maramvaḷarntatu	-maramvaḷarntaṉa
		kaṇcivantatu	-kaṇcivantaṉa

		=ekiṉampuḷ

eḻuttiṉcāriyikaḷ:
taṉimeykaḷeṉṉumcāriyipeṟum.uyirneṭilkaḷkārameṉṉumcāriyipeṟum.aikāraukāraṅkaḷkārameṉṉumcāriyiyuṭaṉkāṉeṉṉumcāriyiyumpeṟum.uyirkkuṟilum,uyirmeykkuṟilumkāramkāṉeṉṉumcāriyiyuṭaṉkarameṉṉumcāriyiyumpeṟum.
eḻuttukaḷiṉpiṟappiṉpotuvilakkaṇam:
oliyeḻuttiṟkuvēṇṭuṅkāraṇaṅkaḷiṟkuṟi
villāmaluyiriṉatumuyaṟciyāluḷḷēniṉṟakāṟṟāṉatueḻuppeḻukiṉṟacevippulaṉāmaṇukkūṭṭam,mārpum,kaḻuttum,taliyummūkkumākiyanāṉkiṭattiyummutaliṟ
porunti,piṉpuutaṭumnākkumpallumvāyumākiyanāṉkiṉuṭiyamuyaṟcivēṟupāṭṭiṉāl,vēṟuvēṟuvakip
paṭṭeḻuttākiyōcikaḷāyttōṉṟutalpiṟappām.
eḻuttumeḻuttuvakiyum:

kaḷiṉkāriyamāyumuḷḷoliyēeḻuttueṉappaṭum.avveḻuttumutaleḻuttucārpeḻuttueṉiruvakippaṭum.
eḻuttukaḷukkuriyamāttirikaḷ:
uyiraḷapeṭikkumāttirimūṉṟu.neṭṭeḻuttiṟkumāttiriiraṇṭu.aikārakkuṟukkattiṟkumāttirioṉṟu.aukārakkuṟukkattiṟkumāttirioṉṟu.oṟṟaḷapeṭikkummāttirioṉṟu.kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkumāttirioṉṟu.meyyeḻut
tiṟkum,kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum,kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟkum,muṟṟāytat

tiṟkum,āytakkuṟukkattiṟkumtaṉittaṉiyēmāttirikālākum.

oṟṟaḷapeṭi:
ceyyuḷilōcikuṟiyumiṭattuccolliṉiṭiyilumkaṭiyilumniṟkumṅ,ñ,ṇ,n,m,ṉ,y,l,v,ḷeṉṉumpattumeykaḷum,āyteḻuttumavvōciyiniṟikkattamaḷavilnīṇṭuolikkum.avvāṟunīṇṭuolippatikkuṟikka,avaṟṟiṉpiṉavvavveḻuttukaḷēaṟikuṟiyākeḻutap
paṭum.ivvāṟuoṟṟeḻuttukaḷtamōciyaḷavilmikuntuolippatuoṟṟaḷapeyaṭiyākum.ivvoṟṟaḷapeṭiorukuṟṟeḻuttiṉkīḻumiraṇṭukuṟṟeḻuttiṉkīḻumvarum.kuṟiliṇikkīḻiṭi,kuṟiṟkīḻiṭi,kuṟiliṇikkīḻkkaṭi,kuṟiṟkīḻkkaṭieṉṉumnāṉkiṭattummēṟkūṟiyapatiṉōreḻuttumaḷapeṭukakum.eṉavēoṟṟaḷapeṭināṟpattunāṉkām.āṉālāytammoḻiyiṉiṭiyilmaṭṭuntāṉvarum.ātalālāytattiṟkumaṭṭumkuṟiliṇikkīḻkkaṭikuṟiṟkīḻkkaṭieṉṉumiraṇṭuiṭattiyumvilakkavēṇṭum.vilakkiṉāloṟṟaḷapeṭināṟpattiraṇṭām.

		maṭaṅṅkalanta
		kaṇṇkaruviḷi
		poṉṉpoṟi

kaṉeṉṉumcolliṟkucciṟappuviti:
kaṉeṉṉumcolmutaṉilittoḻiṟpeyarpōlayakaramallātameykaḷvantālukaraccāriyiporuntumvalliṉamvantālukaraccāriyiyēyaṉṟiakaraccāriyiyumpeṟṟumelleḻuttōṭuvēṟupaṭṭuvarum(varukiṟavalleḻuttāvatuallatuataṟkuiṉamāṉamelleḻuttāvatumikappeṟum)
	(e.ṭu)	kaṉ+naṉmi=kaṉṉunaṉmi
		kaṉ+taṭṭu=kaṉṉattaṭṭu,kaṉṉantaṭṭu-akaram			peṟṟumeṉmiyōṭuuṟaḻntatu.
kālaṅkāṭṭumpakuti:
ku,ṭu,ṟueṉṉummūṉṟuyirmeykaḷiiṟutiyāka
vuṭiyacilakuṟiliṇippakutikaḷvēṟupaṭṭuiṟantakālaṅkāṭṭum.
	(e.ṭu)	pukkāṉ,viṭṭāṉ,peṟṟāṉ.
kāṇṭikiyuriyiṉilakkaṇam:
karutturiyumpatavuriyumeṭuttukkāṭṭumākiyamūṉṟaṉiyuñcollutalālumammūṉṟaṉōṭuviṉā
viṭieṉṉumiraṇṭaṉiyuṅkūṭṭiccollutalālumcūttirattuḷirukkiṉṟaporuḷiviḷakkuvaṉakāṇṭikiyuri
yākum.
kālaṅkāṭṭumiṭinilikaḷ:

kaḷukkuvarum.aviiṟantakālaviṭinili,nikaḻkālaviṭinili,etirkālaviṭinilieṉamūṉṟuvakippaṭum.
kīḻeṉpataṟkucciṟappuviti:

	(e.ṭu)	kīḻ+kuḷam=kīḻkuḷam,kīḻkkuḷam
kuṇacanti:
vaṭacoṟkaḷil,aāviṉmuṉiīvariṉīṟumutaluṅkeṭētōṉṟuvatum,a,āviṉmuṉuūvariṉīṟummutaluṅkeṭōtōṉṟuvatumkuṇacantieṉappaṭum.
	(e.ṭu)	nara+intiraṉ-narēntiraṉ
		cura+īcaṉ-curēcaṉ
		tarā+intiraṉ-tarēntiraṉ
		makā+īcaṉ-makēcaṉ

		kaṅkā+uṟpatti-kaṅkōṟpatti
		tayā+ūrccitaṉ-tayōrccitaṉ.

		kuyiṉ+ciṟumi=kuyiṉciṟumi

kuṟṟiyalukaram:
taṉineṭil,āytam,uyir,valliṉam,melliṉam,iṭiyiṉamākiyivaṟṟuḷoṉṟuīṟṟukkuayaleḻuttāyttoṭarntuvara,valliṉameykaḷiṉmēlūrntuvarumukaramkuṟṟiyalukaramākum.ivvukaramtaṉakkuriyorumāttiriyiṟkuṟintuarimāttiriyākolikkum.
	(e.ṭu)	nāṭu,aḵtu,vayiṟu,cukku,naṇṭu,teḷku
kuṟṟiyalikaram:
irucoṟkaḷpuṇarumpōtuvarumoḻikkumutalilyakaramvara,nilimoḻiīṟṟiluḷḷakuṟṟiyalukaramikaramāyttiriyum.intikaramum,miyāeṉṉummuṉṉiliaciccolliluḷḷikaramumkuṟṟiyalikaraṅkaḷākum.ivvikaramtaṉakkuriyamāttiriyiṟkuṟintuolikkum.
	(e.ṭu)	nāṭu+yātu-nāṭiyātu
		varaku+yātu-varakiyātu
		kēṇmiyācāttā.
kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉvaki:
īṟṟukkuayaleḻuttinōkkakkuṟṟiyalukaramāṟuvakippaṭum.
avi.
	1.neṭiṟṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram	-	nāku
	2.āytattoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram-	kaḵcu

	5.meṉṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram	-	pañcu
	6.iṭittoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram-	cālpu.
kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉmuṉuyirumyakaramumpuṇartal:
kuṟṟiyalukaramāṉatu,varumoḻimutaliluyirvantāltaṉakkuiṭamākiyameyyiviṭṭukkeṭum.yakaramvantālikaramākattiriyum.
	(e.ṭu)	āṭu+aritu-āṭaritu
		nāku+yātu-nākiyātu.

uyireḻuttukaḷila,i,u,e,oeṉṉumintueḻuttukkaḷumkuṟukiyōciyuṭiyaṉavāyolikkiṉṟaṉa.ivaṟṟukkukkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷeṉṟupeyar.ivveḻuttukaḷtaṉittaṉiorumāttiripeṟum.

paṭum.

iṭakkarppeyarākiyapakarīkarāttukkum,nīṅku

paṭarkkippeyarōṭucuṭṭuppeyarcērntuvariṉ,appaṭarkkippeyarmuṭikkuñcoṟkoḷḷumiṭattuataṟkuppiṉvarum;muṭikkuñcoṟkoḷḷāviṭattu,ataṟkuppiṉṉummuṉṉumvarum.

uyireḻuttukaḷilā,ī,ū,ē,ai,ō,aueṉṉumēḻueḻuttukaḷumiraṇṭumāttiripeṟṟunīṇṭōciuṭiyaṉavātalālneṭṭeḻuttukaḷākum.
pakarampiṟakkumiṭam:
mēlutaṭumkīḻutaṭumtammiṟporuntapeṉṉumeḻuttuppiṟakkum.
pakāppatam(patam=col):
pirikkappaṭutaliṉālpayaṉillāmalkāraṇamiṉṟiiṭappaṭṭakuṟiyākittōṉṟiyakālattiliruntēoṉṟākimuṭintunaṭakkiṉṟapeyarccollum,viṉiccollum,iṭiccollum,uriccollumākiyanāṉkumpakāppataṅkaḷākum.
	(e.ṭu)	kāṟṟu	-peyarppakāppatam
		uṇ	-viṉippakāppatam
		kol	-iṭippakāppatam
		uṟu,kaḻi-urippakāppatam.
(tolkāppiyamarapilporuḷkuṟiyāccoletuvumilli)
pakuti(mutaṉili):
peyarppakupataṅkaḷuḷḷumviṉippakupataṅkaḷuḷḷumavvavaṟṟiṉmutalilniṟkiṉṟapakāppataṅkaḷēpakutiyākum.(patam-col)
pakupatavuṟuppukkaḷ:
mutalnili,iṭinili,iṟutinili,cāriyi,canti,vikāramākiyāṟumpakupatavuṟuppukkaḷākum.
pakupatam:
poruḷumiṭamuṅkālamuñciṉiyuṅkuṇamuntoḻiluṅkāraṇamākavarukiṉṟapeyarccoṟkaḷum,teriniliyākavuṅkuṟippākavuṅkālattikkoḷḷumviṉiccoṟkaḷumpakupataṅkaḷākum.
patam:
eḻuttukaḷtāmēovvoṉṟākattaṉittumiraṇṭumutalākattoṭarntumporuḷtarumāyiṉpatamām.appatampakāppatam,pakupatameṉiruvakippaṭum.
pattuṭaṉiraṇṭupuṇartal:
patteṉṉumeṇṇuppeyarmuṉiraṇṭeṉṉumeṇṇuppeyarvarumāyiṉ,patteṉṉumnilimoḻiyiluḷḷatukaravuyirmeykeṭattakarameyṉakarameyyākattiriyum.
	(e.ṭu)	pattu+iraṇṭu=paṉṉiraṇṭu.
pattueṉṉumeṇṇuppeyarmuṉāyirameṉṉumeṇṇuppeyarpuṇartal:
pattueṉṉumeṇṇuppeyarmuṉṉālāyirameṉṉumeṇṇuppeyarvantālumīṟukeṭṭuiṉpeṟṟuiyalpiltiriyātuniṟkum.
	(e.ṭu)	pattu+āyiram=patiṉāyiram.
palacileṉpavaṟṟiṟkucciṟappuviti:
palacileṉṉumivvirucollumtamakkumuṉṉētāmēvarumāyiṉvarumoḻiiyalpātalum,mikutalum,iṟutiyilniṉṟalakarameyṟakarameyyākattiritalum.ivvirucoṟkaḷiṉmuṉṉēvēṟucoṟkaḷiloṉṟuvantālnilimoḻiyiṟutiyilniṉṟaakaramvikaṟpittalumuḷḷaṉavām.

		paṟpala,ciṟcila-akaramkeṭalakaramṟakaramāyiṟṟu.
paṉieṉpataṟkucciṟappuviti:
paṉieṉṉumpeyariṉmuṉkoṭieṉṉumpeyarvarumāyiṉvantakakaramēmikutalum,kacatapakkaḷvarumāyiṉnilimoḻiyīṟṟuaikāramkeṭṭuamcāriyipeṟutalum,tiraḷeṉṉumpeyarvarumāyiṉvantatakarammikuntum,aikeṭṭuamcāriyipeṟṟum,aṭṭueṉṉumpeyarvarumāyiṉnilimoḻiyīṟṟuaikāramkeṭṭuvarumoḻimutaliluḷḷaakaramākāramātalumākum.

melleḻuttukaḷ:
meyyeḻuttukaḷilṅ,ñ,ṇ,n,m,ṉeṉṉumāṟueḻuttukaḷummeṉmiyāṉoliyippeṟṟuḷḷaṉavātalālmelleḻuttukaḷeṉappeyarpeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.ivaṟṟimeli,meṉmi,meṉkaṇameṉavumvaḻaṅkuvar.
meṉṟoṭariṉmuṉiruvaḻiyilumnāṟkaṇamumpuṇartal:

kaḷākattiriyā.

moḻikkuiṟutiyilvarumeḻuttukaḷ:
taṉittum,meyyōṭukūṭiyumvarumpaṉṉiraṇṭuuyireḻuttukaḷum,ña,ṇa,na,ma,ṉa,ya,ra,la,va,ḻa,ḷeṉṉumpatiṉōrumeyyeḻuttukaḷum,kuṟṟiyalukaramumākiyirupattunāṉkueḻuttukaḷummoḻikkuiṟutiyilvarumeḻuttukaḷākum.
moḻikkumutalilvarumeḻuttukaḷ:
a,ā,i,ī,u,ū,e,ē,ai,o,ō,aueṉṉumpaṉṉiraṇṭuuyireḻuttukaḷum,ka,ca,ta,na,pa,meṉṉumāṟumeyeḻuttukaḷumpaṉṉiraṇṭuuyireḻuttukaḷōṭukūṭivarutalālavieḻupattiraṇṭumvakarameyeṭṭuuyirōṭukūṭivarutalālavieṭṭum,yakarameyāṟuuyirōṭukūṭivarutalālaviāṟum,ñakaramnāṉkuuyirōṭukūṭivarutalālavināṉkumṅakarameyōruyirōṭukūṭivarutalālatuoṉṟumākanūṟṟumūṉṟueḻuttukaḷēmoḻikkumutalākumeḻuttukaḷākum.
moḻipuṇariyalpu:
nilimoḻiyiṉiṟutiyōṭuvarumoḻimutalporunta,peyarccollōṭupeyarccollippuṇarkkuṅkālattum,peyarccollōṭuviṉiccollippuṇarkkuṅkālattum,viṉiccollōṭupeyarccollippuṇarkkuṅkālattum,viṉiccollōṭuviṉiccollippuṇarkkuṅ
kālattumtiriyumiṭammūṉṟum,iyalpuoṉṟumākiyanāṉkēmoḻikaḷtammiṟpuṇarumiyalpākum.

		maṇ+yāppu=maṇṇiyāppu-vēṟṟumi.

yaraḻeṉṉummūṉṟumeykaḷiṉmuṉmayaṅkum
eḻuttukaḷ:
yaraḻeṉṉummūṉṟumeykaḷiṉmuṉṉēkacatapaṅañanameṉkiṉṟeṭṭumeykaḷumuyirmeyyākimayaṅkutalu

vaṭamoḻitamiḻilvarumpoḻuturakarattimutalilē
yuṭiyamoḻikkua,i,ueṉṉummūṉṟukuṟṟeḻuttukaḷiloṉṟum,lakarattimutalilēyuṭiyamoḻikkui,ueṉṉumeḻuttukaḷiloṉṟum,yakarattimutalilēyuṭiyamoḻikkuikaramummoḻikkumutalākivarum.mēlumvaṭamoḻiyuḷiraṇṭeḻuttuiṇintuōreḻuttippōlnaṭakkumpōtupiṉniṉṟayakara,rakara,lakaraṅkaṭkuikaramummakaravakaraṅkaṭkuukaramum,nakarattiṟkuakaramummuṉṉēvarum.

valliṉam:
	k,c,ṭ,t,p,ṟākiyāṟumeyyeḻuttukaḷumvaṉmiyāṉōciyippeṟṟuḷḷatālvalliṉameyyeḻuttukaḷākum.ivveḻuttukaḷmārpiiṭamākakkoṇṭupiṟakkum.
vaḻānili:
coṟkaḷtoṭarumiṭattumuṭikkappaṭumcoṟkaḷōṭumuṭikkumcoṟkaḷtiṇi,pāl,iṭam,kālam,viṉā,viṭi,marapueṉṉumivvēḻilummāṟupaṭāmaltoṭarntuniṟpatumuṟiyākum.avvāṟutoṭarntuniṟpaṉavaḻānilieṉappaṭum.
vaḻinūl:
muṉivaṉceytamutalnūlvaḻiyēataṉioṭṭicceyyappaṭuvatuvaḻinūlākum.mutalnūlāciriyaṉvirittucceytatittokuttukkūṟutalum,avaṉtokuttukkūṟiyavaṟṟivirittukkūṟutalum,avviruvakiyiṉiyumtokiviriyākakkūṟutalumpiṟamoḻinūlimoḻipeyarttuttamiḻiṉāṟceytalumeṉavaḻinūlnāṉkuvakippaṭum.

viṉāveḻuttukaḷ:
e,yā,ā,ō,ēeṉṉumintueḻuttukaḷumcoṟkaḷilamintuviṉāpporuḷittarumāṉālviṉāveḻuttukaḷākum.ivaṟṟuḷe,yāeṉpaṉacollukkumutalilamintuviṉāpporuḷittarum.ā,ōeṉpaṉacollukkuiṟutiyilamintuviṉāpporuḷittarum.ēcollukkumutalilumiṟutiyilumniṉṟuviṉāpporuḷittarum.mēlumivviṉāakaviṉāpuṟaviṉāeṉiruvakippaṭum.

iṭittoṭarum,āytattoṭarum,oṟṟuiṭiyēmikātaneṭiṟṟoṭarum,uyirttoṭarumākiyivaṟṟiṉmuṉvarukiṟavalliṉamvēṟṟumippuṇarcciyiliyalpākum.
	(e.ṭu)	mārpu+kaṭumi=mārpukaṭumi-iṭittoṭar
		eḵku+ciṟumi=eḵkuciṟumi-āytattoṭar
		nāku+tīmi=nākutīmi-neṭiṟṟoṭar
		varaku+katir=varakukatir-uyirttoṭar
vēṟṟumippuṇarcciyiloṟṟuiṭiyēmikumneṭiṟṟoṭar:
neṭiṟṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaramoḻiyilukaramēṟiniṉṟaṭakara,ṟakarameykaḷvēṟṟumippuṇarcciyilperumpālumiraṭṭum.
	(e.ṭu)	āṭu+kāl-āṭṭukkāl
		cōṟu+vaḷam-cōṟṟuvaḷam
vēṟṟumippuṇarcciyiloṟṟuiṭiyēmikumuyirttoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram:
uyirttoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaramoḻiyilukaramēṟiniṉṟaṭakaraṟakarameykaḷvēṟṟumippuṇarcciyilperumpālumiraṭṭum.
	(e.ṭu)	muraṭu+maṉitaṉ-muraṭṭumaṉitaṉ
		vayiṟu+iṭi-vayiṟṟiṭi
ḻakarampiṟakkumiṭam:
mēlvāynuṉiyinākkiṉnuṉiyāṉatutaṭavaḻakarampiṟakkum.
ḻakaravīṟṟiṉmuṉvalliṉampuṇartal:
ḻakarameyyiṉmuṉkacatapakkaḷvariṉalvaḻiyileḻuvāyttoṭar,ummittoki,viṉittokiākiyavaṟṟiliyalpātalum,paṇputtokiuvamittokikaḷilmikutalum,vēṟṟumiyilmikutalum,valliṉamāvatumelliṉamāvatumikutalumvitiyākum.
	(e.ṭu)	vīḻ+tītu=vīḻtītu-alvaḻiyiliyalpāyatu.
		yāḻ+karuvi=yāḻkkaruvi
		ūḻ+payaṉ=ūḻppayaṉ
		kumiḻ+kōṭu=kumiḻkkōṭu
				kumiḻṅkōṭu
ḻakaravīṟuvēṟṟumiyilpeyarvaṉkaṇattōṭupuṇartal:
ḻakaravīṟṟuppeyarvaṉkaṇamvantālvēṟṟumikkaṇrakāravīṟṟuiyalpiṟṟāyvalleḻuttumikkumuṭiyum.
(e.ṭu)pūḻkkāl,pūḻcciṟaku.
ḷeṉṉummeyyeḻuttiṉmuṉmayaṅkummeykaḷ:
ḷakarameyyiṉmuṉkacapavayeṉṉumintumeykaḷummayaṅkum.
	(e.ṭu)	veḷki,nīḷcili,koḷpa,kēḷvi,veḷyāṉi.
ḷakarattiṟkucciṟappuviti:
taṉikkuṟṟeḻutticcārntaḷakaramalvaḻiyilvarumṭakaramtirintaviṭattuṭakaramākattiritalēyaṉṟiāytamākavuntiriyum.
	(e.ṭu)	muḷ+tītu=muṭṭītu,muḵṭītu.
ḷakarampiṟakkumiṭam:
mēlvāyināviṉōramtaṭittuttaṭavutalālḷakarampiṟakkum.
ḷakaravīṟṟuppuṇarcci:
ḷakarattiṉmuṉvalliṉamvarumāyiṉvēṟṟumiyilum,alvaḻiyilpaṇputtokiyilum,uvamittokiyilumiṟutiḷakaramṭakaramākattiriyum.eḻuvāyttoṭarilumummittokiyilumtiriyātiyalpākum.
	(e.ṭu)	muḷ+kuṟi=muṭkuṟi-valliṉamvara					vēṟṟumiyilṭakaramākattirintatu.
		aruḷ+celvam=aruṭcelvam-alvaḻiyil-			paṇputtokiyilṭakaramākattirintatu.
		vāḷ+kaṇ=vāṭkaṇ-valliṉamvaraalvaḻiyil			-uvamittokiyilṭakaramākattirintatu.
		poruḷ+peritu=poruḷperitu-eḻuvāyttoṭar
		poruḷ+pukaḻ=poruḷpukaḻ-ummittoki
		valliṉamvaraalvaḻiyileḻuvāyttoṭarummittokiākiyavaṟṟilḷakarameyiyalpāyniṉṟaṉa.
		aruḷpeṟṟāṉeṉiraṇṭāmvēṟṟumittokiyilum,vāḷpōḻntiṭṭāṉeṉamūṉṟāmvēṟṟumittokiyilumvarumoḻiviṉiyāyaviṭattuttiriyāveṉakkoḷka.
ḷakaravīṟṟuppuṇarcci:
moḻiyiṟutiyiluḷḷaḷakarameyvalliṉamvantālvēṟṟumiyilṭakarameyyākattiriyum.alvaḻiyilṭakaramākattirintumtiriyāmalumvikaṟpittalumākum.vēṟṟumialvaḻiiraṇṭilummelliṉamvantālṇakarameyyākattiriyum.iṭiyiṉamvariṉiyalpākum.
	(e.ṭu)	muḷ+kuṟi=muṭkuṟi-vēṟṟumiyilvalivara			ṭakaramākattirintatu.

muḷ+valitu-muḷvalitu

ṉakarampiṟakkumiṭam:

2.col

aṭimaṟimāṟṟupporuḷkōḷ:
poruḷukkuēṟṟiṭattileṭuttukkūṭṭumaṭiyiṉiyuṭiyatum,ētēṉumōraṭiyieṭuttuacceyyuḷiṉmutal,iṭi,kaṭiākiyētēṉumōriṭattilkūṭṭiṉālumporuḷuṭaṉōcimāṭciyum,poruṭciṟappumvēṟupaṭātaaṭiyiṉiyuṭiyatumākiyaporuḷkōḷēaṭimaṟimāṟṟupporuḷkōḷeṉappaṭum.
	(e.ṭu)	māṟākkātalarmalimaṟantaṉarē;
		āṟākkaṇpaṉivaralāṉāvē;
		ēṟāmeṉṟōḷvaḷinekiḻummē;
		kūṟāytōḻiyāṉvāḻumāṟē
itaṉuḷevvaṭiyieṅkēkūṭṭiṉālumporuḷumōciyumvēṟupaṭāmiyiaṟiyalām.
aṭukkuccol:

	(e.ṭu)	uṇṭēṉuṇṭēṉ,pōpōpō-virivu
		eyyey,eṟieṟieṟi-vekuḷi
		varukavaruka;polikapolikapolika-uvaki
		uyyēṉuyyēṉ;vāḻēṉvāḻēṉvāḻēṉ-									tuṉpam.
aṭimoḻi:
poruḷiṭam,kālam,ciṉi,kuṇam,toḻileṉṉumāṟum,iṉamuḷḷaporuḷkaḷukkēyaṉṟiiṉamallāpporuḷkaḷukkumulakavaḻakkuceyyuḷvaḻakkuākiyiraṇṭiṭattumaṭimoḻikaḷākavarum.

	(e.ṭu)	kalippāvilkāṇka.
aṇmi:

yuṇṭātaliaṟiyalām.
	poṟukkieṉṟucolliiṭiveḷiviṭṭuppiṉṉareṭuttamāṇikkameṉṟālvaciyā?iciyā?

		paḻaṅkaṇōṭṭamutaliyavaḻuṅkiṉaṉallaṉō
aḷimaṟipāppupporuḷkōḷ:
pāmpu,puṟṟilnuḻiyumpoḻututalimēlākumpaṭinilimāṟutaliyalpu.avvāṟēīṟṟuaṭiyilīṟṟilniṉṟacolataṉiṭiyilummutalilumceṉṟuporuḷkoḷḷaamivatuaḷimaṟipāppupporuḷkōḷākum.aḷi=puṟṟu;pāppu=pāmpu.
(e.ṭu)	tāḻntuṇarviṉarāyttāḷuṭintu
		taṇṭūṉṟittaḷarvārtāmum
	cūḻntaviṉiyākkicuṭaviḷintu
		nāṟkatiyilcuḻalvārtāmum
	mūḻntapiṇinaliyamuṉcey
		viṉiyeṉṟēmuṉivārtāmum
	vāḻntapoḻutiṉēvāṉeytu
		neṟimuṉṉimuyalātārē
	icceyyuḷilvāḻntapoḻutiṉēvāṉeytuneṟimuṉṉimuyalātāreṉṉumīṟṟaṭimūḻnta....muṉivār,cūḻnta...cuḻalvār,tāḻnta...taḷarvāreṉattalikīḻākiṭiyilummutalilumceṉṟaṭitaliaṟika.
aṉmoḻittoki:
vēṟṟumittoki,viṉittoki,paṇputtoki,uvamittoki,ummittokiākiyaintutokinilittoṭaruntattamporuḷpaṭumaḷaviṟkuttokātutattamakkuppuṟattētāmallātapiṟamoḻipporuḷpaṭattokuvatuaṉmoḻittokiyākum.
(e.ṭu)
1.	pūṅkuḻali-iraṇṭāmvēṟṟumittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.itupūviyuṭiyakuḻaliḷiyuṭiyāḷeṉaviriyum.
	poṟṟoṭiyāḷ-mūṉṟāmvēṟṟumittokippuṟattupapiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.itu,poṉṉālākiyatoṭiyiṉiyuṭiyāḷeṉaviriyum.
	aṇiyilakkaṇam-nāṉkāmvēṟṟumittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.ituaṇikkuilakkaṇameṉaviriyum.
	poṟṟāli-aintāmvēṟṟumittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻit
toki.itupoṉṉiṉākiyatāliyiyuṉiṭayāḷeṉaviriyum.
	cēraṉkuṭi-āṟāmvēṟṟumittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.itucēraṉatukuṭimakkaḷirukkumūreṉaviriyum.
	kīḻvayiṟṟukkaḻali-ēḻāmvēṟṟumittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.itukīḻvayiṟṟiṉkaṇeḻuntakaḻalipōlvāṉeṉaviriyum.
2.	tāḻkuḻali-viṉittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.tāḻntacūḻaliṉiyuṭiyāḷeṉaviriyum.
3.	karuṅkuḻali-paṇputtokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.karumiyāṉakūntaliuṭiyavaḷeṉaviriyum.
4.	tēṉmoḻiyāḷ-uvamittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻit
toki.tēṉpōlummoḻiṉiyuṭiyāḷeṉaviriyum.
5.	uyirmey-ummittokippuṟattuppiṟantaaṉmoḻittoki.uyirummeyyumkūṭippiṟanteḻuttueṉaviriyum
aḵṟiṇi
tāḻvākiyapakuppu,vilaṅku,paṟavimutaliyuyiruḷḷa
vaṟṟiyumnilam,nīr,kalmutaliyuyiraṟṟavaṟṟiyumkuṟikkum.oṉṟaṉpāl,palaviṉpālākiyiraṇṭupālkaḷumittiṇikkuuriyapālkaḷākum.al+tiṇi=aḵṟiṇiuyartiṇiallātatiṇi
	(e.ṭu)	kaḷiṟu-oṉṟaṉpāl

aḵṟiṇippalaviṉpāṟpaṭarkkippeyarkaḷ:

āṟaṟivuyir:
uṭampiṉālum,vāyiṉālum,mūkkiṉālum,kaṇṇiṉālum,ceviyiṉālumaṟiyumaṟivōṭuepporuḷiyumpakuttaṟiyumaṟiviyumpeṟṟiruppatuāṟaṟivuyirākum.
(e.ṭu)	makkaḷ,tēvar,narakar,acurar,iyakkarmutalāyiṉōr.
makkaḷtāmēāṟaṟivuyirēeṉpatutolkāppiyaneṟi,puṉivuneṟipaṟṟit,tēvarnarakaracurariyakkareṉoṭṭavittatu.naṉṉūl.
āṟāmvēṟṟumiurupukaḷēṟkumpeyarkaḷ:
āṟāmvēṟṟumiurupukaḷ,tammiyēṟṟapeyarp
poruḷivarumoḻipporuḷākiyataṟkiḻamipporu
ḷōṭumpiṟitiṉkiḻamipporuḷōṭuntoṭarpuṭiyaporuḷākavēṟupaṭuttum.avvāṟuvēṟupaṭṭacampantapporuḷēivvurupukaḷiṉporuḷām.
	(e.ṭu)	cāttaṉki-taṟkiḻami
		cāttaṉpottakam-piṟitiṉkiḻami
		kiḻami-urimi.
āṟāmvēṟṟumiyiṉurupukaḷ:

yorumippeyariyum,aurupuaḵṟiṇippaṉmippeyariyumkoḷḷum.ivvurupukaḷniṟṟaṟkuriyaviṭaṅkaḷiluṭiyeṉpatucollurupākavantuirutiṇiyorumippaṉpuppeyarkaḷiyuṅkoḷḷum.

		taṉātuki

		cāttaṉuṭiyavīṭu,cāttaṉuṭiyavīṭukaḷ.

umeṉṉumiṭiccol:
umeṉṉumiṭiccoletirmaṟiyum,iruvakic
ciṟappum,aiyamum,iruvakieccamum,muṟṟum,eṇṇum,teriniliyum,ākkamumākiyeṭṭupporuḷittarum.
(e.ṭu)	
etirmaṟi:cāttaṉvarutaṟkumuriyaṉ-itilvārāmikkumuriyaṉeṉapporuḷpaṭutalāletirmaṟi.
uyarvuciṟappu:kuṟavarumañcuṅkuṉṟu-itilkuṟavarmukamākakkuṉṟiṉuyarviviḷakkutalāluyarvuciṟappu.
iḻivuciṟappu:puliyaṉumvirumpātayākki-itilpuliyaṉmukamākuṭampiṉiḻiviviḷakkutalāliḻivuciṟappu.
aiyam:avaṉvelliṉumvellum-itil,tuṇiyāmiyiuṇarttutalāliyam.
iṟantatutaḻuviyeccam:iṉṟumkoṟṟaṉvantāṉ.itil,muṉkoṟṟaṉvantatallāmaleṉṉumporuḷittantatāliṟantatutaḻuviyeccam.
etiratutaḻuviyeccam:koṟṟaṉumvaruvāṉeṉṉumporuḷittantatāletiratutaḻuviyeccam.

1.uṇṭēmaṟumi:-ituteḷivupporuḷittarutalāl		tēṟṟam.
2.nīyēkoṇṭāy?-itilnīyākoṇṭāyeṉaviṉavutaṟporuḷittarutalāṟṟalviṉā.
3.nilamēnīrētīyē-itilnilamumnīrumtīyumeṉeṇṇutalāleṇ.

6.ēyēivaḷoruttipēṭi-itilvēṟuporuḷillāmalceyyuḷiliciyiniṟittuniṟṟalāliciniṟi.
7.iyampuvaṉeḻuttē-itilvēṟuporuḷillāmaliṟutiyilniṟṟalālīṟṟaci.
