vieṉṉumuriccoluṇarttuṅkuṟippu:
vieṉṉumuriccolkūrmieṉṉuṅkuṟippiyuṇarttum.vi=nel;ataṉnuṉikkūrmiyāluriyāyiṟṟu.
	(e.ṭu)	vinuṉippakaḻi

3.akapporuḷ
akattiṇi:-
uḷḷattēnikaḻumiṉpavoḻukkam.atukikkiḷi,kuṟiñcittiṇi,pālittiṇi,mullittiṇi,marutat
tiṇi,neytaṟṟiṇi,peruntiṇieṉēḻuvakippaṭum.
akattiṇiyiyal:-
iṉpamākiyoḻukkattiṉilakkaṇam,iṉpavilakkaṇam,akattiṇiāvatuatu.
akattiṇikkuriyamutal(avatti)nilimeyppāṭukaḷ:-
pukukiṉṟamukattiṉivirumputal,neṟṟiviyarviyiaṭiyappeṟutal,nakiyuṇṭātalimaṟittal,maṉamaḻitalippiṟarkkuppulaṉākātumaṟittalākiyanāṉkumakattiṇik
kuriyamutalnilimeyppāṭukaḷākum.
akattiṇikkuriyiraṇṭāmniliyiṉmeyppāṭukaḷ:-
kūntaliaviḻttal,kātilaṇintaaṇiyikkīḻēviḻacceytuataṉittēṭuvatippōlaniṟṟal,muṟiyākaaṇintuḷḷaaṇikaḷittaṭavutal,āṭiyikkulittuuṭuttalākiyanāṉkumiraṇṭāvatuniliyiṉmeyppāṭu
kaḷākum.
akattiṇikkuriyamūṉṟāmniliyiṉmeyppāṭukaḷ:-
alkulittaṭavutal,aṇintiruntaaṇikaḷikkaḷintuaṇital,tammiccāraniṉittārittamatuiṟpiṟappuccollippuṇartaṟkuicivillārippōlamaṟuttukkūṟutal,vēṭkimikutiyālnāṇaḻitalilmuyaṅkalviruppattālirukikaḷiyummēlētūkkutalākiyanāṉkummūṉṟāmniliyiṉmeyppāṭukaḷākum.
akattiṇikkuriyanāṉkāmnilimeyppāṭukaḷ:
pārāṭṭikkūṟutal,peṇkaḷiṉiyalpākiyamaṭamikeṭumāṟucilakūṟutal,ūriluḷḷavarkaḷum,cēriyiluḷḷavarkaḷumkūṟumaruḷillātakūṟṟikkēṭṭualarāyiṟṟeṉanāṇutal,talimakaṉkoṭukkuṅkiyuṟipporuḷikkoḷḷutal,ākiyanāṉkumnāṉkāmniliyiṉmeyppāṭukaḷākum.
akattiṇikkuriyaintāmniliyiṉmeyppāṭu:
ārāyntuuṭampaṭutal,vēṭkinalitalālviḷiyāṭ
ṭuttoḻilimaṟattal,talivaṉikkāṇṭalvēṭkiyālmaṟintoḻukutal,talimakaṉikkaṇṭavaḻimakiḻtaleṉṟanāṉkumintāmnilikkuriyameyppāṭukaḷākum.
akattiṇikkuriyāṟāmniliyiṉmeyppāṭu:
pūvuñ,cāntum,tukilum,mutaliyaṉakoṇṭupuṟattēaḻakuceyyaakattēcitivuṇṭātalum,aḻakiḻantutōṉṟutalum,kiyuṅkaḷavumākappiṭipaṭṭakaḷvarippōṉṟucolluvaṉavaṟṟittaṭumāṟṟantōṉṟaccollutal,kalaṅkātucolluṅkālceyalaṟavutōṉṟaccollutalākiyanāṉkumāṟāmnilikkuriyameyppāṭukaḷākum.
akattiṇikkunimittamātal:
akattiṇikkēuriyaveṉṟukūṟappaṭṭaṉirupattunāṉkumeyppāṭukaḷākum,nōkkāminōkkiiṉpuṟutal,nōkkuṅkālicceṟṟārpōlanōkkutal,maṟintu
kāṇṭal,taṟkāṭṭuṟuttalpōṉṟanilikaḷpattumavaṟṟōṭuporuntinilipeṟṟaviṉiyiuṭiyanimittameṉṟukūṟuvar.mēlēkūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷintiṇikkaṇvaruvaṉāṟumkūṟappaṭṭatu.ēḻāvatuniliperuntiṇippāṟpaṭum.eṭṭāvatuniliuṉmattam.oṉpatāvatunilimayakkam.pattāvatunilicākkāṭu.
akattiṇikkuriyapāṭalkaḷ:
nāṭakavaḻakkiṭattum,ulakiyalvaḻakkiṭattumuḷḷaakapporuḷkaruttukaḷākiyakikkiḷimutalākapperun
tiṇiiṟutiyākaamittukkūṟupavikaḷikkalippā,paripāṭalākiyiruvakippākkaḷālumkūṟappaṭum.nāṭakavaḻakkāvatu-cuvipaṭavaruvaṉavellāmōriṭattuvantaṉavākattokuttukkūṟutal,ulakiyalvaḻakkāvatu-ulakattāroḻukalāṟṟōṭuottukkūṟuvatu.
akappāṭṭuṟuppu:
akattiṇiyiṉpaṉṉiraṇṭuṟuppukaḷ.aviiyalpakat
tiṇi,vakiyakattiṇi,potuvakattiṇi,ciṟappakattiṇi,uvamavakattiṇi,puṟaniliyakattiṇi,etirniliyakattiṇi,kāraṇamallatukaruviyakattiṇi,kāriyavakattiṇi,kārakavakat
tiṇi,muṉṉaviyakattiṇi,piṉṉaviyakattiṇieṉpaṉa
vām.
akappuṟakkikkiḷi-
kāmañcālāiḷamittaṉmiyuṭiyatalimakaḷiṭat
tiltalimakaṉavaḷuṭiyakuṟippiṉiyaṟiyātupalavaṟṟikkūṟuvatu.
akappuṟattiṇi:
akamākiyaintiṇikkumpuṟaṉākiyoḻukkam.atukikkiḷiyumperuntiṇiyumeṉiruvakippaṭum.(kik
kiḷi-orutalikkāmam.peruntiṇi-poruntākkāmam)
akappuṟappāṭṭu:
maṭalēṟutalmutalākattaputāraniliyīṟākaccollap
paṭṭavaṟṟiliyaṟpeyarkōṭāmiyām.
akappuṟapperuntiṇi
akaṉṟuḻikkalaṅkalmutalākattaliviyuntāṉumvaṉa
maṭintunōṟṟalīṟākaccollappaṭṭaṉavumpiṟavuṅ
kūṟutalām.
akappuṟam:
kikkiḷiyumperuntiṇiyum;avikāntaḷ,vaḷḷi,curanaṭi,mutupāli,tāpatam,taputāram,kuṟṟici,kuṟuṅkali,illāṇmulli,pācaṟimullieṉṉumpattunukarvukaḷ.
akapporuṭkikkiḷi-
orutalikkāmam.aḵtāvatukāmamnukartaṟkamintiḷamiyiyuṭiyatalimakaḷiṭattuṇṭāyakuṟippiṉittāṉaṟiyumaḷavumtalimakaṉavaḷiccārātuniṉṟuneñcoṭukūṟuvatu.
akapporuḷukkucciṟappuviti:-
makkaḷiṉakavoḻukkattivariyaṟuttuuṇarttumakapporuḷviḷakkamintiṇiyiṭattumkūṟappaṭumpuṇartalmutaliyoḻukkattiṉtalippaṭṭārippotuppeyarālkūṟuvatallatuciṟappākoruvarpeyariyumamittukkūṟutaleṉpatuilli.
akapporuḷ:
aivakiilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷoṉṟuaḵtuṇṇikaḻcciyāṉaciṟṟiṉpattiṟkuriyēḻutiṇiyikkūṟum.ēḻutiṇikaḷ:kuṟiñci,mulli,marutam,neytal,pāli,kikkiḷi,perun
tiṇi.
akapporuṭṭuṟi:
akappāṭṭuṟuppukaḷpaṉṉiraṇṭaṉuḷoṉṟu.aḵturippōruṅkēṭpōrumiṉṟippulavarkūṟṟākakkūṟappaṭuvatu.
akapporuṭperuntiṇi:
akattiṉkaṇṇuḷḷiṉpatticcilanāḷnukarntatalivaṉuntaliviyumpiṉiṭikkālattunukarāmikkāṉiṭiyūṟṟukkūṟṟukkaḷiuṇarntukūṟuvatu.
accam:-
itueṇvakimeyppāṭukaḷuḷoṉṟu.aṇaṅku,vilaṅku,kaḷvar,aracareṉṉumnālvakiyāṉumaccan
tōṉṟum.ivaṟṟuḷaṇaṅkeṉpaṉa.pēyum,pūtamum,pāmpum,īṟākiyapatiṉeṇkaṇaṉumnirayappālarumpiṟarumaṇaṅkutaṟṟoḻiliṉarākiyacavantiṉpeṇṭirmutaliyaṉavumām.
aḻuki:-
itueṇvakimeyppāṭukaḷuḷoṉṟu.iḻivum,iḻat
talum,acitalum,vaṟumiyumeṉṟanāṉkiṭattumaḻukitōṉṟum.ivaṟṟuḷiḷiveṉpatupiṟarālikaḻappaṭṭueḷiyaṉātaliḻattaleṉpatu,tantiyuntāyumākiyacuṟṟat
tāriyum,iṉpantuykkumnukarccimutaliyavaṟṟiyumiḻattal.acitaleṉpatutaḷarcci;taṉṉiliyiṟṟāḻtal.vaṟumiyeṉpatuiṉpantuykkātapaṟṟuḷḷam.
eṭu:-
ilvaḻaṅkumaṭamayilpiṇikkuñ
	colvalivēṭṭuvaṉāyiṉaṉmuṉṉē(puṟam.252)
ituacivupaṟṟittōṉṟiyaavalam.
iṉṉaviṟalumuḷakolnamakkeṉa
	mūtiṟpeṇṭirkacintaḻanāṇik
	kūṟṟukkaṇṇōṭiyaveruvarupaṟantali.
ituiḻattalpaṟṟivantaavalam
aṟattoṭuniṟṟal-
taliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkattimuṟiyēveḷippaṭuttiniṟṟal.aṟatoṭuniṟṟalākummuṟiyēveḷippaṭuttiniṟṟa
lāvatu,talivitōḻikkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ.tōḻicevilittāykkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ.cevilinaṟṟāykkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ.
naṟṟāytantitaṉṉiyarkkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ.
taṉiyar=taṉuṭaṉpiṟantār.
aṟattoṭuniliyiṉvaki:
muṉṉilimoḻi,muṉṉilippuṟamoḻieṉiraṇṭuvakiyiṉiyuṭiyatu.aṟattoṭunili.muṉṉilimoḻi
yāvatumuṉṉiṟpārkkunērēkūṟuvatu.muṉṉilippuṟamoḻiyāvatumuṉṉiṟpārkkukkūṟavēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟippiṟarukkukkūṟuvārpōlakkūṟuvatu.
aṟattoṭunilinikaḻumiṭam:
talimakaṉvarumvaḻiyiluṇṭākiyatuṉpattiṟkuañciyum,tamarvarivuetirkoḷḷātaviṭattum,vēṟṟārtirumaṇampēcivarumiṭattum,talivikkukkāvalmikunta
viṭattumaṟattoṭunilinikaḻum.
aṟattoṭuniṟṟaṟkuriyār
talivitōḻikkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ,tōḻicevilit
tāykkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ,cevilittāynaṟṟāykkuaṟat
toṭuniṟpāḷ,naṟṟāytantitamiyaṉmutalāṉōrkkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ.
aṟattoṭuniṟpārkkuviṉānikaḻumiṭam:
talimakaḷiṭattilvēṟupāṭukaṇṭavaḻitōḻiavaḷatuvēṟupāṭṭiṟkukkāraṇaṅkēṭpāḷ.cevilittāytōḻiyikkēṭpāḷ.naṟṟāycevilittāy,tōḻiākiyiruvariyum,taliviyiṉvēṟupāṭṭiṟkukkāraṇaṅkēṭpāḷ.akkālattuaṟattoṭuniṟkumniliuṇṭākumaṟamāvatukātalkoṇṭāṉimaṇattal.aṟivarurimi:aṟivareṉpavartalimakaṉukkuntalimakaḷukkummaṉattiṇmiyippayakkumaṟivurikaḷivaḻaṅkumuyarntakuṇattiṉiuṭiyacāṉṟōrāvar.
iṭantalippāṭu
iyaṟkippuṇarccipuṇarntuceṉṟatalimakaṉaṭuttanāḷavviṭattēvantutalimakaḷikkūṭutaliṭantalippāṭām.atuteyvanteḷital,kūṭutal,viṭuttaleṉamūṉṟuvakiyiṉiyuṭiyatu.ivaṟṟuḷteyvanteḷitalāvatu,muṉṉēnamakkavaḷikkūṭṭivittateyvampiṉṉumkūṭṭivikkumeṉatteḷintucellutal.kūṭaleṉpatutalivaṉtaliviyikkūṭutalām.viṭuttaleṉpatutalivaṉtaliviyiāyattiṉpāṟcellaviṭuttalām.
iṭantalippāṭṭiṉilakkaṇaviri
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyilavaḷittantavitiiṉṟumavviṭattiṟkucceṉṟāltarumeṉaccērtalum,iyaṟkippuṇarcciyilkaṇṭatupōltaliviyikkāṇṭalum,puṇartalum,puṇarntapiṉputaliviyittalivaṉpukaḻntukūṟutalum,āyakkūṭṭattuḷtaliviyicceluttutalumākiyaintuviriyiṉiyuṭiyatuiṭantalippāṭu.
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉilakkaṇam:
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇnikaḻumnālvakippuṇarcciyuḷiyaṟkippuṇarcciyumoṉṟu.aḵtāvatuūḻiṉāltalimakaṉtalimakaḷietirppaṭaavviruvarummaṉamiyintutāmēkūṭuvatām.appuṇarcciteyvattāṟpuṇarumpuṇarcci,talimakaḷāṟpuṇarumpuṇarccieṉiruvakippaṭum.teyvat
tāṟpuṇarumpuṇarccimuyaṟciyiṉṟimuṭivatākum.tali
makaḷāṟpuṇarumpuṇarccimuyaṟciyālmuṭivatākum.
iyaṟpeyarvaruvataṟkuiṭam:
ilakkiyaṅkaḷiloruvariṉpeyariccuṭṭikkūṟutalpuṟattiṇiyiṭattuvarappeṟum.
ilvāḻkki:
talivaṉumtaliviyumilliṉkaṇvāḻumvāḻkkiyiṉmēṉmiyikkūṟutalilvāḻkkiyākum.itukiḻavōṉmakiḻcci,kiḻattimakiḻcci,tōḻimakiḻcci,cevilimakiḻccieṉanāṉkuvakippaṭum.
iravukkuṟi:
talivaṉtaliviyiiravuppoḻutilkuṟittiṭattilkūṭutaliravukkuṟiyākum
iravukkuṟiyiṉvaki:
vēṇṭal,maṟuttal,uṭaṉpaṭal,kūṭṭal,kūṭal,nīṅkal,pārāṭṭal,uyaṅkal,pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭaleṉiravukkuṟioṉpatuvakippaṭum.
vēṇṭal-talivaṉiravukkuṟiyivēṇṭutalum,tōḻitaliviyivēṇṭutalumām.maṟuttal-tōḻiyum,taliviyumiṟiyōṉvēṇṭukōḷimaṟuttal;uṭaṉpaṭal-tōḻitalivaṉvēṇṭukōṭkuuṭaṉpaṭutalum,talivitōḻiyiṉcoṟkuuṭaṉpaṭutalumām.kūṭṭal-tōḻitaliviyiaḻittucceṉṟukuṟiyiṭattuccērttal.kūṭal-talivaṉtaliviyippuṇartal;pārāṭṭal-talivaṉtaliviyippukaḻtalum,tōḻiiṟivaṉaḷittakiyuṟiyippukaḻtalumām.pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭal-talivaṉtaliviyivīṭṭiṟkuccellaviṭuppattōḻitaliviyiaḻittuccellutal;uyaṅkal-talivaṉiravukkuṟikkaṇvarumvaḻiyiṉatuarumiyieṇṇittalivivaruntutalum,ataṉāltalivaṉvaruntikkūṟutalumām.nīṅkal-tōḻitaliviyikkuṟikkaṇceluttinīṅkutalumtaliviyittalivaṉpuṇarntunīṅkutalumām.
iravukkuṟiyiṉviri:
talivaṉtaliviyiiravukkuṟikkaṇkūṭavirumpittōḻiyiṭamiravukkuṟivēṇṭutalum,talivaṉiravukkuṟik
kaṇvarumvaḻiyiṉatuarumiyittōḻitalivaṉukkukkūṟutalum,talivaṉiravukkuṟikkaṇvarumvaḻiyiṉatueḷimiyikkūṟutalum,tōḻitalivaṉnāṭṭuppeṇkaḷaṇiyumaṇiyiyum,puṉital,aṇitalviḷiyāṭṭiṭammutaliyavaṟṟiyumviṉātalum,tōḻiyiṉkuṟippaṟintuavaḷnāṭṭuaṇiiyalittalivaṉviṉātalum,tōḻitaṉṉāṭṭuaṇiyiyalikkūṟutalum,tōḻitalivaṉiōriṭattuniṟuttittaliviniṟkumiṭañceṉṟutalimakaṉkuṟiyiyaṟivittalum,ivvāṟutalivaṉkuṟiyittōḻikūṟakkēṭṭataliviuṭaṉpaṭātutaṉneñcoṭukūṟutalum,ivvāṟuneñcoṭupulantuniṉṟatalivipiṉpāṅkiyōṭuuṭaṉpaṭṭukkūṟutalum,taliviiruṭkuṟikkuuṭaṉ
paṭṭamiyippāṅkitalivaṉukkukkūṟutalum,tōḻitalivaṉikkuṟiyiṭattuniṟuttittāyiṉatuuṟakkattiaṟitalum,talivikkuttalivaṉvaravippāṅkiuṇarttalum,tōḻitalimakaḷikkuṟiyiṭattukkoṇṭucellutalum,talimakaḷikkuṟiyiṭattucceluttittōḻiakaṉṟupōtalum,talivaṉtaliviyietirppaṭutalum,talivaṉvarumvaḻiyieṇṇittalimakaḷvaruntutalum,talivaṉtaliviyittēṟṟutalum,talivaṉtaliviyikkūṭalum.taliviyittalivaṉpukaḻtalum,iruṭkuṟiyiṉētattiyañcittalimakaḷtalimakaṉiiravukkuṟikkaṇvaru
talivilakkalum,talivaṉtaliviyivīṭṭiṟkuccelleṉaviṭuttalum,tōḻitaliviyiaṭintukiyuṟikāṭṭalum,taliviyittōḻivīṭṭiṟkukkūṭṭippōtalum,tōḻitalimakaḷivīṭṭilcērttapiṉputalimakaṉiṭañceṉṟuivviruḷilnīvāraleṉṟuvaravuvilakkikkūṟutalum,tōḻiivvāṟuiravukkuṟivilakkiyacoṟkēṭṭuttalimakaṉmayaṅkikkūṟutalum,pāṅkitalimakaṉukkuttalimakaḷiṉtuyarattikkūṟinīrūrpōyccērntaceytiyiyāmaṟiyumāṟukuṟiceykeṉṟukūṟiviṭuttalum,talimakaṉtaṉṉūrcērtalumākiyirupattēḻumiravukkuṟiyiṉvirikaḷākum.
iravukkuṟiiṭiyīṭu;-
eṭṭāmnāḷiravukkuṟikkaṇvantatalimakaṉallakuṟippaṭutalāliṭiyīṭupaṭṭuppōtal.allakuṟippaṭutalāvatu-talivaṉumtaliviyumtōḻiyumtaṅkaḷukkuḷkuṟittukkoṇṭapaṭitalivaṉālnikaḻttappaṭutaṟkuriyapaṟaviyeḻupputal,nīriṟkalleṟital,iḷanīrviḻacceytalmutaliyakuṟikaḷpiṟitoṉṟaṉālnikaḻttappaṭattalivikuṟikkaṇvantutalivaṉikkāṇātuceṉṟāḷ.piṟakutalivaṉaṅkuvantutaliviyippārāmalceṉṟāṉ.ivvāṟutalivaṉālnikaḻttappaṭutaṟkuriyakuṟikaḷpiṟitoṉṟaṉālnikaḻttappaṭutaliṉaḵtuallakuṟiyāyiṟṟu.
iravukkuṟiiṭiyīṭṭiṉviri:
talivikkuttōḻitalivaṉkuṟiyiṭattuvantamiyiuṇarttalum;talivitāṉallakuṟiyiṭattuniṉṟutalivaṉikkāṇāmiyāltirumpiviṭiyaṟkālattuvantupāṅkiyuṭaṉkūṟutalum;tōḻitalivaṉceytatīṅkieṭuttukkūṟutalum,talivaṉtāṉkuṟittakuṟiyiliruntutaliviallakuṟippaṭṭuvārāmiyāṟvaruntitaṉūrkkuccellutalum,oṉpatāmnāḷpoḻutuviṭintapiṉtalivivaṟuṅkaḷaṅkaṇṭutuṉpa
maṭitalum,talivitaṉtuṉpattittōḻikkurittalum,talimakaḷatutuṉpattippāṅkitaṇittukkūṟutalum,oṉpatāmnāḷiruṭkuṟikkaṇvantatalivaṉmēlpāṅkiallakuṟippaṭṭakuṟṟamēṟṟikkūṟutalum,talivimēltalivaṉallakuṟippaṭṭakuṟṟamēṟṟikkūṟutalum,talivikuṟimayaṅkiyatittōḻitalivaṉukkuuṇarttalum,talivaṉkūṟiyakoṭumiyittōḻitalivikkuṇarttalum.kuṟi
piḻittatueṉpiḻiyaṉṟueṉṟutalivivaruntikkūṟutalu
mākiyapaṉṉiraṇṭutuṟikaḷumiravukkuṟiiṭiyīṭṭiṉallakuṟippaṭutaliṉvakikkuriyavirikaḷākum.tāyuṟaṅkātuviḻittiruttal,tāytūṅkiyapiṉūrilirukkumnāykaḷuṟaṅkāmi,nāyuṟaṅkiyaviṭattuūriluḷḷamakkaḷuṟaṅkāmi,ūrāruṟaṅkiyaviṭattunakarkāppōrtuṭiyaṭittukkoṇṭuvirintuūrcuṟṟivarutal,talivaṉiruṭkuṟikkaṇvarutaṟkuiṭiyūṟākanilavuveḷippaṭutal,kūkikuḻaṟakkēṭṭatalivipayantukūṟutal,talivaṉtaliviiruvarkkumiṭiyūṟāykkōḻikūvutalākiyēḻumiravukkuṟiiṭiyīṭṭiṉvaruntoḻiṟkarumieṉṉumvakik
kuriyavirikaḷākum.
irakkamoṭumīṭci:-
ituvumuṭaṉpōkkiṭiyīṭṭiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.talivaṉtaṉṉiviṭṭunīṅkiṉamiyiṉtalivivaruttattuṭaṉtamarpiṉtaṉmaṉikkumīṇṭuvarutalirakkamoṭumīṭciyām.
iruvarumuḷvaḻiavaṉvaravuṇartal:
ituvumpāṅki(tōḻi)matiuṭaṉpāṭueṉṉumkaḷaviyalkiḷavittokikkuriyavakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.taliviyumpāṅkiyumoruṅkiruntapōtutalimakaṉtaḻiyumkaṇṇiyuṅkoṇṭuvantuūr,peyarmutaliyaṉaviṉāyaviṭattuttōḻiataṟkuviṭiyaḷittalum,talivaṉappāṟceṉṟaviṭattuttaliviyinōkkiacatiyāṭinakutalum,pāṅkitaṉaṟiviṉtiṟattāltalivaṉtaliviākiyiruvaratumaṉakkuṟippiārāytalumākiyivikaḷālkūṭṭamuṇmiyippāṅkiuṇartalām.
ilvāḻkkiyiṉviri:-
talivaṉtalivimuṉpāṅkiyippukaḻntukūṟutalum,talivaṉippāṅkivāḻttikkūṟutalum,pāṅkitaliviyittirumaṇanāḷaḷavumtuṉpappaṭāmaliruntavāṟṟikkūṟukaveṉṟalum,talivaṉaḷittakiyuṟipporuḷiyētuṇiyākakkoṇṭuvaruntātuiruntēṉeṉṟutalivikūṟutalum,talivaṉippāṅkitirumaṇanāḷaḷavumnilipeṟa
vāṟṟiyanilimiyiviṉātalum,kaliyāṇamaṉiyilvantacevilittāykkuttōḻitalivaṉtaliviākiyiruvaratuaṉpumuṟavumuṇarttalum,talivaṉtaliviilvāḻkkiciṟappākirukkiṟatueṉṟupāṅkicevilittāykkurittalum,maṇattiṟkucceṉṟuvantacevilitaliviyippeṟṟanaṟṟāykkuttalivaṉtaliviyiṉciṟappikkūṟutalumcevilinaṟṟāykkuttalimakaṉilvāḻkkiyiṉtaṉmiyikkūṟutalum,cevilinaṟṟāykkuttalivaṉtaliviākiyiruvaratukātaliyumaṟivittalumākiyapattumilvāḻkkiyiṉvirikaḷākum.
iḷavēṉiṟkālattiṉtaṉmi:
cittiri,vikāciākiyirumātaṅkaḷumiḷavēṉiṟ
kālamākum.ipparuvattilmaṉmataṉtērākiyateṉṟalvīcum.vaṇṭiṉamum,kiḷiyum,pūviyum,aṉṟilum,kuyilummakiḻcciyuṭaṉirukkum.māṅkaṉiyutirumnīrmalaraṉṟittāḻi,puṉṉi,ceṇpakammutaliyavimalarum.kārpparuvattumakiḻntamayilpōṉṟavimeliyum.iviyēikkālattiṉtaṉmikaḷākum.
iḷivaral:-
itueṇvakimeyppāṭukaḷiloṉṟu.mūppum,piṇiyum,varuttamum,meṉmiyumeṉṟanāṉkiṉporuṭṭiṉālumiḷivaraltōṉṟum.ivaṟṟuḷmūppueṉpatutaṉṉiṭattuttōṉṟiyamūppukāraṇamākattōṉṟumiḷivaralum,piṟaṉkaṭtōṉṟiyamūppukāraṇamākattōṉṟumiḷivaralumeṉiraṇṭām.piṇieṉpatutaṉkaṇtōṉṟiyapiṇikāraṇa
mākattōṉṟumiḷivaralumpiṟaṉkaṭtōṉṟiyapiṇikāraṇamākattōṉṟiyiḷivaralumeṉiraṇṭām.varuttameṉpatutaṉkaṭtōṉṟiyavaruttaṅkāraṇamākattōṉṟumiḷivaralum,piṟaṉkaṭtōṉṟiyavaruttaṅ
kāraṇamākattōṉṟumiḷivaralumeṉiraṇṭām.meṉmiyeṉpatumelliyalpukāraṇamākattōṉṟumiḷivaral.iḷivaral-iḻivu.
uṭaṉpōkku:
itutaliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkattiveḷippaṭuttaṟ
kuriyakiḷavittokikaḷuḷoṉṟu.taliviyiṉcuṟṟattārkkutteriyāmaltalivaṉtaliviyittaṉṉūrkkuuṭaṉkoṇṭucellutal.
muṉṉilippuṟamoḻi:-
muṉniṟpārkkukkūṟavēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟippiṟarukkukkūṟuvārpōlakkūṟuvatumuṉṉilippuṟamoḻiyākum.
uṭaṉpōkkiṉvaki:
pōkkaṟivuṟuttal,pōkkuṭaṉpaṭāmi,pōk
kuṭaṉpaṭuttal.uṭaṉpaṭutal,pōkkal,vilakkal,pukaḻtal,tēṟṟaleṉuṭaṉpōkkueṭṭuvakippaṭum.
uṭaṉpaṭutal:
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.tōḻiyiṉaṟivuriyiṉpaṭitaliviyuntalivaṉumuṭaṉpōkkiṟkuuṭaṉpaṭutal.
uṭaṉpōkkunikaḻntaviṭattuaṟattoṭuniṟṟaṟkuuriyār:-
talimakaḷum,talimakaṉumpuṇarntuṭaṉpōya
kālattuttōḻiyum,ceviliyum,naṟṟāyumaṟattoṭuniṟpārkaḷ.
uṭaṉpōkkiṉviri:-
talivikkukkāvalmikutiyākirukkiṉṟatu.ākiyālavaḷiuṉūrkkuuṭaṉkoṇṭucelvāyākeṉṟupāṅkitalimakaṉukkukkūṟutalum,pāṅkiyiṉkūṟṟiṟkuuṭaṉpaṭatalimakaṉmaṟuttalum,pāṅkitalimakaṉiuṭaṉpaṭumāṟuceytalum,taliviyiuṭaṉkoṇṭucellattalivaṉuṭaṉpaṭutalum,talivaṉuṭaṉpōtalippāṅkitalivikkuurittalum,uṭaṉpōkkiṉālnāṇaḻiyumēeṉṟataṟkuttaliviyiraṅkikkūṟutalum,ivvāṟuvaruntiyatalivikkuttōḻikaṟpiṉmēmpāṭṭikkūṟutalum,talivitalivaṉuṭaṉpōtaṟkuuṭaṉpaṭutalum,tōḻipālinilattiṉtaṉmiyikkūṟiyaviṭattuttalivimaṟumoḻikūṟutalum,pāṅkitalimakaḷittalimakaṟkuaṭikkalamkoṭuttalum,pāṅkitaliviyittalivaṉuṭaṉiruḷceṟintiṭiyāmattilviṭuttalum,talimakaḷittalimakaṉpālinilattil(curattil)celuttutalum,tali
makaḷpālinilattilcellutalālēṟpaṭṭatuṉpattittalimakaṉāṟṟuvittalum,talimakaḷimakiḻntuavaḷkūntaṟkumalariccūṭṭi,ataṉālparavayamakiḻcciyaṭintukūṟutalum,talivaṉtaliviyarkaḷiṉtōṟṟattikkaṇṭuivarkaḷmāṉiṭarkaḷā?allatutēvarkaḷāeṉakkaṇṭōriyamuṟṟukkūṟutalum,talivaṉtaliviyarmītukoṇṭaaṉpiṉālpālinilattiluḷḷeyiṟṟiyarāṭavarmutalāyiṉōravarkaḷatucelavivilakkittaṅkaḷpāṭiyiltaṅkippōṅkaḷeṉṟukūṟutalum,ivvāṟukaṇṭōrkūṟiyataṟku,ivviṭattilnumpatiyiltaṅkippōtalporuntātu,eṉpatippōtalvēṇṭumeṉṟutalivaṉkūṟiyavaḻit,talivaṉtaṉpatiarukilirukkiṉṟa
teṉpataṉikkaṇṭōrkūṟutalum,talimakaṉtalimakaṭkuttaṉpaticērntamikūṟutalumākiyapatiṉeṭṭumuṭaṉpōkkiṉvirikaḷākum.
uṭaṉpōkkiṭiyīṭu:-
taliviyiṉcuṟṟattiṉarnamillattiṉkaṇmaṇantukoḷḷāmaltaṉṉūriṉkaṇmaṇantukoṇṭaṉaṉeṉṟuveṟuppaṭitaliaṟintutalivaṉtaliviyiuṭaṉkoṇṭupōkiyiltaliviyiṉcuṟṟattārpiṉtoṭarntucellattalimakaḷittalivaṉviṭuttuccellutaluṭaṉ
pōkkiṭiyīṭākum.itupōkkaṟivuṟuttal,varavuaṟivuṟuttal,nīkkam,irakkamoṭumīṭcieṉanāṉkuvakippaṭum.
uṭaṉpōkkiṭiyīṭṭiṉviri:-
aimpattintānāḷ,taṉūriviṭṭunīṅkuntalivietirvaruvārtammoṭutalivaṉuṭaṉuṭaṉpōkkuccellutalippāṅkiyarkkuuṇarttiviṭuttalum,talimakaḷtaṉcelaviantaṇarmūlamnaṟṟāykkukkūṟiviṭuttalumacceytiyinaṟṟāykkuantaṇarmoḻitalum,antaṇarkūṟṟikkēṭṭanaṟṟāymuṉṉilippuṟamoḻiyāltaliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkattittaliviyiṉtamarkkuaṟattoṭuniṟka,tamarciṉantukuḻāṅkoṇṭupiṉcēṟalittalivikaṇṭutalivaṟkuṇarttalum,taliviyiṉtamarpiṉvarutaliyaṟintu
talivaṉtaliviyiviṭuttuccellutalum,tamaruṭaṉcelluntalivitalivaṉpuṟaṅkāṭṭippōtalinōkkikkavalippaṭṭuttēṟutalumākiyāṟumuṭaṉpōkkiṭi
yīṭṭiṉvirikaḷākum.
uripporuḷ:
puṇartalum,puṇartalnimittamum,piritalum,piritalnimittamum,iruttalum,iruttalnimittamum,ūṭalum,ūṭalnimittamum,iraṅkalum,iraṅkalnimittamumeṉṟapattuvakiyiṉiyuṭiyatām.
uvaki:-
itueṇvakimeyppāṭukaḷuḷoṉṟu.celvanukarcci,mey,vāy,kaṇ,mūkku,ceviākiyaimpulaṉkaḷālnukarumnukarcci,makaḷiroṭupuṇartal,cōliyumāṟumpukuntuviḷiyāṭumviḷiyāṭṭuākiyanālvakiyāṉumuvakitōṉṟum.
uḷḷappuṇarccinikaḻuṅkālam:
paḻipāvaṅkaḷikkaṇṭuañcutalum(kuṟṟantaruñceyal
kaḷicceyyāmiyum)aṟivuṭiyavaṉākiruttalumtali
makaṉukkunilipeṟṟakuṇaṅkaḷātalālum,accam,nāṇ,pētamiākiyamūṉṟuntalimakaḷukkunilipeṟṟakuṇaṅ
kaḷātalāṉumuḷḷappuṇarcci,meyyuṟupuṇarccieṉṉumiruvakippuṇarcciyuḷḷumuḷḷappuṇarccimuṉṉarnikaḻum.
ivaṟṟuḷ,accameṉpatukāṇātatoṉṟukaṇṭāṟpeṇṭiriṭattunikaḻvatu.nāṇameṉpatupeṇṭirkkuiyalpākiyakuṇam.tirunutalnāṇueṉpatuatu.
ūṭalittaṇikkumvāyilkaḷ:
pāṇaṉ,pāṭiṉi,kūttar,iḷiyar,kaṇṭōr,pākaṉ,pāṅkaṉpāṅki,cevili,aṟivar,kāmakkiḻatti,kātaṟputalvaṉ,viruntiṉar,āṟṟāmiākiyivarkaḷtalivaṉtaliviyarkaḷukkiṭiyēēṟpaṭumūṭalinīkkuvārkaḷ.
eṇvakimaṇam(āriyavaḻippaṭṭatu):
piramam,piracāpattiyam,āriṭam,teyvam,kāntaruvam,acuram,irākkatam,picācameṉpaṉāriyeṇvakimaṇaṅkaḷākum.ivaṟṟuḷpiramamāvatu-nāṟpatteṭṭuāṇṭukaḷpiramaccariyaṅkāttavaṉukkuppaṉṉiraṇṭuyāṇṭuparuvattaḷāyppūppuaṭinteḻilāriḷanaṅkiyiiraṇṭāmpūppueytāmikkumuṉpēaṇikalaṉaṇintutāṉamākakkoṭuppatām.piracāpattiyamāvatu-talimakaṉkuravarvēṇṭattalimakaḷkuravarmaṟātuuṭaṉpaṭṭuavarkaḷkoṭuttaparicuttokikkuiraṇṭumaṭaṅkākakkoṭuttuttaṉmakaḷiyumoppaṉiceytutīmuṉṉarkkoṭuppatām.teyvamāvatuvēḷviyāciriyaṉukkukkaṉṉiyialaṅkarittuttīmuṉṉarkkoṭuppatām.āriṭamāvatuoṉṟāṉumiraṇṭāṉumāvumāṉēṟumvāṅkikkoṇṭukaṉṉiyioppaṉiceytutīmuṉṉarkkoṭuppatām.
kāntaruvamāvatu,koṭuppārumkēṭpārumiṉṟittalimakaṉuntalimakaḷuntaṉiyiṭattetirppaṭṭuttāmēkūṭu
vatām.acuramāvatutalimakaṭkuppoṉpūṭṭiccuṟṟat
tārkkuvēṇṭuvaṉakoṭuttukkoḷvatuirākkatamaṇa
māvatutaṉṉiṉumtamariṉumpeṟātuvalitiṟkoḷvatām.picācamāvatumūttōr,kaḷḷuṇṭukaḷittōr,tuyiṉṟōrākiyavarkaḷiṭattuppuṇartalum,iḻintavaḷimaṇañ
ceyvatumpiṟavumām.
immaṇaṅkaḷuḷpiramam,piracāpattiyam,āriṭam,teyvamākiyanāṉkumperuntiṇikkuriyamaṇaṅkaḷākum.kāntaruvamintiṉikkuriyatu.acuram,irākkatam,picācamākiyamūṉṟumaṇaṅkaḷumkikkiḷikkuuriyaṉavām.
aintiṇikkumuriyakikōḷ:
kuṟiñci,mulli,marutam,neytal,pāliākiyaintiṇiyiṭattukkaḷavum,kaṟpumeṉiraṇṭoḻukkamnikaḻum.
aintiṇikkumuriyaporuḷkaḷ:
kuṟiñci,mulli,marutam,neytal,pāliākiyaintiṇikaḷum,mutaṟporuḷ,karupporuḷ,uripporuḷākiyamūṉṟuporuḷāṉuñcollappaṭum.
aintiṇioḻukkattiṟkupporuntātavar:
piṟarukkuaṭimittoṇṭuceytuvāḻvōrum,piṟarēvalvaḻiniṉṟuceyalceypavarum,piṟarālēvutaṟkuriyārumintiṇioḻukkattiṟkupporuntār.avarkaḷakappuṟattiṇiyākiyakikkiḷikkum,peruntiṇikkumuriyarāvar.mēlumaṟam,poruḷ,iṉpamkeṭamakaḷirikkātalikkumtalimakkaḷumakappuṟattiṇiyākiyakikkiḷikkumperun
tiṇikkumuriyavarāvar.aintiṇioḻukkattiṟkuuriyavarevarkkumetaṟkumaṭimippaṭāttammurimiyarāyiruntālaṉṟiavarvāḻvuciṟappuṟātueṉpatukoṇṭavitiiḵtām.
aiyanteḷital;
taliviyiṉkūntalilvaṇṭukaḷmoyttalālum,aṇikaḷaṇintiruppatālum,mārpu,tōḷmutaliyavaṟṟilpaccikkuttiyiruppatālum,acitalālum,kaṇimit
talālum,āṇmakkaḷikkaṇṭuañcutalālum,kālkaḷnilattiltōytalālum,viyarttalālum,niḻalāṭutalālum,mālivāṭutalālumtalimakaṉiyamnīṅkiivaḷmāṉiṭamakaḷēeṉṟutuṇivāṉ.
eṭu:-
tirunutalvērarumpuntēṅkōtivāṭum
	irunilañcēvaṭiyuntōyu-mariparanta
	pōkitaḻuṇkaṇumimikkum
	ākumaṟṟivaḷakaliṭattaṇaṅkē.
aintiṇiyilmaṉaṉaḻiyātaniliyilnikaḻummeyppāṭukaḷ:
kaḷavuiṭiyīṭupaṭṭaviṭattuvaruntātuivvāṟākiniṉṟateṉattalivaṉiiṭittukkūṟutal;veṟuppiṉippiṟarkkuttōṉṟātavāṟuuṭaliṉkaṇṇēniṟuttal;kaḷa
voḻukkampiṟarālaṟiyappaṭumeṉakkūṭṭattiṉakaṉṟuoḻukutal;puṇarcciyivilakkutal;tūtuviṭṭapoḻutuciṉaṅ
koḷḷāmi;uṟaṅkipporuntutal;kātalaḷavukaṭantuvarutal;uḷḷakkaruttiṉippuṟattēkūṟātumaṟittiruttal,teyvattiṉiañcutal;aṟattiṉittērntuteḷital;talimakaṉiṭattuillātakuṟippiṉiavaṉmāṭṭuiruppatākayeṇṇiveṟuttal;talivaṉceykiṉṟataliyaḷiyiveṟuttal;maṉanikaḻcciyuṇmiyikkūṟutal;poḻutiṉimaṟuttal;talimakaṉmāṭṭuaruḷpulappaṭaniṟkumnili;aṉpuveḷippaṭaniṟṟal;piriviṉippoṟāmi;kaḷavoḻukkattikkūṟiyapuṟañcol;ataṟkuuḷḷamnāṇutalākiyayāvumintiṇiyilmaṉaṉaḻiyātaniliyilnikaḻummeyppāṭukaḷākum.
aiyam:
talimakaṉtalimakaḷittaṉiyēnalviṉivayattāletirppaṭṭaviṭattu,avaḷvaṭivum,avaḷiavaṉkaṇṭiṭamumciṟappuṭiyāyakālattuttalimakaṉiṭattu,ivaḷmakkaḷuḷḷāḷallaḷ,teyvamō?eṉattalivimāṭṭuaiyamnikaḻum.
eṭu:-
aṇaṅkukolāymayilkollōkaṇaṅkuḻi
	mātarkolmālumeṉneñcu.
orucārpakaṟkuṟi:
pakaṟkuṟiyiṭattuttaliviyippuṇarntuceṉṟatalimakaṉmaṟṟināḷtaṉvēṭkimikutiyālpakaṟ
kuṟiyiṭattuvantuniṟkap,pāṅkikuṟiyiṭattuttaliviyicceluttātumaṟuttukkūṟattalivaṉvarunticcellutalorucārpakaṟkuṟiyākum.itaṉiorukūṟṟuppakaṟkuṟieṉavuṅkūṟuvar.
orucārpakaṟkuṟiyiṉvaki:-
iraṅkal,vaṉpuṟi,iṟceṟippuākiyamūṉṟumorucārpakaṟkuṟiyiṉvakikaḷākum.
iraṅkal-talivaṉpirintapoḻututalivivaruntutalum,atukaṇṭupāṅkipulamputalum,taliviyippuṇarātatalivaṉvaruntutalumām.vaṉpuṟi-taliviyippāṅkiiṭitturittal.iṟceṟippuuṇarttal-taliviyiveḷiyilpōkātavāṟuvīṭṭiṉkaṇṇēceṟittamiyippāṅkit
talivaṉukkuṇarttal.
orucārpakaṟkuṟiyiṉviri:
talivaṉtaliviyippuṇarātuceṉṟaviṭattuttali
viyikkāmanōyvaruttamālippoḻutinōkkiyum,pāṅkiyinōkkiyum,taṉṉuḷḷattinōkkiyum,varuntikkūṟutal.ivvāṟutalivikūṟiyatikkēṭṭapāṅkivaruntutal.talivaṉtaliviyiccantikkāmalnāḻikinīṭṭittaviṭattuttalivivaruntikkūṟutal.pāṅki,nīivvāṟuvaruntuvatumuṟimiyaṉṟueṉattaliviyiiṭitturittal.ivvāṟuiṭittukkūṟiyapāṅkimuṉṉālniṟkaavaḷmēlveṟuppālavaḷinōkkikkūṟātupuṟamoḻiyākakkūṟutal.veṟuppālmuṉṉilippuṟamoḻikēṭṭapāṅkitaliviyiyupacarit
talālveṟuppunīṅkippāṅkiyoṭutalivikūṟutal.pāṅki,nīivvāṟuvaruntiṉālavvaruttattālnalaṉaḻiyum,nalaṉaḻiyavēaṉṉikkuaiyantōṉṟum.ataṉāliṟceṟippuvarum.maṟṟavarmaṇampēcivantālumvaruvar,ataṉālkaṟpukkeṭumeṉṟutaliviyiaccamuṟuttikkūṟutal.
talivaṉviṭṭunīṅkaṟkuarumiyittaliviniṉintutaṉṉuḷvaruntikkūṟutal.talivaṉvaravippāṅkitalivikkuaṟivittal.talivaṉvēlippuṟamākakkuṟiyiṭattuvarattōḻitaṅkaḷukkuḷḷiṟceṟippiaṟivuṟuttakkūṟutal.talivaṉmuṉṉiliyākaniṟkattōḻitalimakaṉippārkkātavaḷpōlappuṟamoḻiyāyppaṟavikaḷinōkkikkūṟuvāḷpōṉṟuiṟceṟippiaṟivuṟuttal.pāṅkitalimakaṉikkuṟivilakkal.ivvāṟupāṅkikuṟivilakkiṉā
ḷāyiṉumemmimaṟavāmivēṇṭumeṉṟukūṟutal.taliviyittalivaṉpuṇarāmiyāltaṉneñcoṭuvaruntikkūṟutalākiyapatiṉāṉkumorucārpakaṟkuṟikkuriyakiḷavikaḷākum.
oruvaḻittaṇattal:-
varivukūṟiyatōḻiyōṭuvaritaṟkuuṭaṉpaṭṭa
talivaṉtaṉūrkkuoruvaḻippōyvarukiṟēṉeṉṟukūṟippōtaloruvaḻittaṇattalākum.
taṇattal=pirital.
oruvaḻittaṇattaliṉvaki:
talivaṉtāṉoruvaḻittaṇantucellumcelavippāṅkikkutterivittalum,avaḷataṉittalivikkuaṟi
vittalumākiyacelavaṟivuṟuttalum,talivaṉcelavippāṅkitaṭuttalākiyacelavuṭaṉpaṭāmiyum,talivaṉtāṉcellavēṇṭiyatuiṉṟiyamiyātatueṉṟukūṟippāṅkiyiuṭaṉpaṭacceytalākiyacelavuṭaṉpaṭuttalum,talivaṉcelvataṟkuppāṅkiuṭaṉpaṭalākiyacelavuṭaṉpaṭutalum,talivaṉoruvaḻittaṇantapōtutalivimaṉaṅkalaṅkivaruntutalākiyaceṉṟuḻikkalaṅkalum,tōḻitaliviyiṉmaṉanteḷiyumpaṭiyāṉacoṟkaḷiccolliavaṟṟālavaḷtuṉpamāṟumpaṭiceytalākiyatēṟṟiyāṟṟuvittalum,mīṇṭuvantatalivaṉpāṟpāṅkivaruntikkūṟutalum,taṉpiriviṉāltalivikkumtōḻikkumuṇṭāyatuṉpattiṟkākattalivaṉvaruntikkūṟutalumākiyavantuḻinonturiyumākiyēḻumoruvaḻittaṇattaliṉvakikaḷākum.
oruvaḻittaṇattaliṉviri:
talivaṉtaṉṉāṭṭiṟkuoruvaḻittaṇantucellavirumpiyatittōḻikkukkūṟutalum,tōḻitalivaṉcelavittaṭuttalum,talivaṉtāṉcellavēṇṭiyataṉiṉṟiyamiyāmiyikkūṟiccelaviṟkuuṭaṉpaṭumāṟukūṟutalum,pāṅkitalivaṉiūrkkuppōyvarukaveṉaviṭuttalum,talivaṉcelavippāṅkitalivikkuṇarttalum,talivaṉcelaviyaṟintatalivivaruntutalum,talivaṉmālikkālamaḷavumvārātuvaravunīṭittalāṟkāmamikuntavataṉālmikuntaniṉivōṭukūṟutalum,tōḻitalivikkuttuṉpamnīṅkumpaṭikūṟutalum,talivaṉcelavumīṇṭamitalivikkukkūṟutalum,mīṇṭuvantatalivaṉiṭamtāmpaṭṭapirivuttuṉpattikkūṟutalum,ataṟkuttalivaṉnīvirevvaṇṇamāṟṟiyiruntīreṉaviṉātalum,taliviyiāṟṟuvittiruntaarumiyittōḻitalivaṉukkukkūṟutalu
mākiyapaṉṉiraṇṭumoruvaḻittaṇattaliṉvirikaḷākum.
ōtaṟpirivu:
kalvikaṟpataṟkākappiriyumpirivuōtaṟpirivākum.ip
pirivumūṉṟiyāṇṭukālavelliyiṉiuṭiyatu.ippiriviṉkaṇceṉṟatalimakaṉōtutaliviṭṭuvarutaṟkuvirumputalum,talimakaḷiniṉittuppulamputalumkūṭā.
kaṇṭōrirakkam:
talivaṉuṭaṉuṭaṉpōkkucceṉṟataliviyiṉtōḻi
mārkaḷumtāyumvaruntutalkaṇṭōriraṅkikkūṟutal.
karaṇamtōṉṟakkāraṇam:
maṇamakkaḷiṭiyēpoykūṟutalum,kuṟṟappaṭanaṭattalumtōṉṟiyapiṉṉartamiḻakaccāṉṟōrtirumaṇattiṟkuriyacaṭaṅkukaḷivariyaṟiceytaṉar.aḵtāvatu,makaḷiraḻakilmayaṅkiavarippaṉmuṟiyilciṟappittukkūṟiavarkaḷkātalippeṟṟuppuṇarntapiṉpuavarimaṟantuvēṟupeṇkaḷināṭumāṭavarum,kūntalnarippiṉumkoṅkitaḷariṉumuṉṉikkiviṭēṉeṉṟuuṟutimoḻikūṟi
viṭṭuppiṉṉarkiviṭumāṭavarumtōṉṟiyapiṉṉarttamiḻakaccāṉṟōrtirumaṇattiṟkuriyacaṭaṅkukaḷivakuttaṉar.
karupporuḷiṉvaki:-
teyvam,makkaḷ,puḷ,vilaṅku,ūr,nīr,pū,maram,uṇavu,paṟi,yāḻ,paṇ,toḻileṉṟapatiṉāṉkumintiṇikkumuriyakarupporuḷkaḷiṉvakikaḷākum.
karupporuṭkucciṟappuviti:
ētēṉumorunilattiṟkuriyamalarumpaṟaviyumantanilattiṟkumataṟkuriyakālattiṟkumporuntātupiṟitorunilattiṭattuvariṉum,vantanilattiṟkuriyakarupporuḷākavēkoḷḷappaṭum.
kavalmaṉimaruṭci:-
taṉṉuṭiyapeṇkātalaṉuṭaṉceṉṟataṉiuṇarntunaṟṟāyvīṭṭiṉkaṇiruntuvaruntutalkavalmaṉimaruṭci.
kavalmaṉimaruṭciyiṉviri:
naṟṟāycakuṉappaṟavikaḷivaṇaṅkutalum,naṟṟāypālinilattiṉvemmikuḷirumāṟukūṟutalum,taṉmakaḷiṉmelliyiyalpākiyataṉmikkunaṟṟāyvaruntutalum,naṟṟāytaliviiḷamittaṉmikkumaṉamelintuiraṅkikkūṟutalum,naṟṟāytaliviyatuaccattaṉmiyieṇṇittāṉaccamuṟṟuiraṅkutalumākiyaintumkavalmaṉimaruṭciyiṉvirikaḷākum.
kaḷaviṉilakkaṇam:-
iṉpamum,poruḷum,aṟamumeṉṟucollappaṭṭamūṉṟaṉuḷ,aṉpuṭaṉpuṇarnta(kikkiḷiperuntiṇinīṅkiya)aivakioḻukkattiṉiṭattumnikaḻumkāmakkūṭṭamāriyamuṟiyilvariyaṟiceyyappaṭṭeṇvakimaṇattuḷ,kāntaruvamaṇattōṭuottiyalpiṉiyuṭiyatām.kāntaruva
māvatu,koṭuppārumkēṭpārumiṉṟittalimakaṉumtalimakaḷumtaṉiyiṭattetirppaṭṭuttāmēkūṭuvatām.aḵtāvatukaḷavuneṟieṉpatām.
kaḷaviṟkukkāraṇam:-
oruvaṉumoruttiyumillaṟamnaṭattumiṭattu,avviru
variyummaṟupiṟappiṉumkūṭṭuvatumpirippatumākiyanalviṇitīviṉiyākiyiruvakiūḻiṭattum,iruvaruḷḷamumpiṟapputōṟumoṉṟupaṭṭunalviṉikkaṇṇēnikaḻntūḻiṉatuāṇiyāl,piṟappu,celvam,aṟivu,aḻakumutaliyapattukkuṇaṅkaḷiṉālumoṉṟupaṭṭatalivaṉum,taliviyumvaṭakaṭa
liṭṭorunukamorutuḷiteṉkaṭaliṭṭorukaḻiceṉṟukōttāṟpōlavum,veṅkatirkkaṉaliyum,taṇkatirmatiyamumtaṉvaḻimāṟioṉṟukūṭiṉāṟpōlavumoṉṟukūṭioruva
roruvarikkāṇṭalkāraṇamākattamiyarāyetirppaṭuvar.ikkaruttiṉināṟkavirācanampikāṭciyeṉṉuntuṟiyilamittukkūṟuvar.
kaḷaviṉvaḻivantakaṟpiṟpuṇarccikkoruciṟappilakkaṇam:
kaḷaviṉvaḻivantakaṟpu,kaḷaviṉvaḻivārākkaṟpueṉṟiraṇṭaṉuḷkaḷaviṉvaḻivantakaṟpiṟpuṇarcci,talivitalivaṉuṭaṉcērntuuṭaṉpōkkiṟceṉṟaviṭattukkoṭuttaṟkuriyataliviyiṉcuṟṟattārillātaviṭattuccaṭaṅkoṭumaṇañceytukoḷḷutaliṉāl,talimakaḷcuṟṟattārāṟpeṟappaṭātakōṭpāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyatām.
kaḷaviṟpuṇarcciyiṉvaki:
iyaṟkippuṇarcci,iṭantalippāṭu,pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam,pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭameṉanālvakiyālkaḷaviṟpuṇarccinaṭipeṟum.
kaḷavoḻukkiṉkaṇnikaḻumpiriviṉvaki:
oruvaḻittaṇattal,variviṭivittupporuḷvayiṟpiritaleṉṟiraṇṭumkaḷavoḻukkiṉnikaḻumpirivukaḷām.ivaṟṟuḷoruvaḻittaṇattalāvatu,ōrūriṉkaṇṇum,orunāṭṭiṉkaṇṇumpiritalām.ippiriviṉkaṇtalimakaṉkāṭṭikkaṭantum,nāṭṭikkaṭantumcellāṉākiyālitaṟkukkālavelliilli.variviṭivittupporuvayiṟpiritalāvatu,tirumaṇattiiṭiyilēvittuattirumaṇattiṟkuvēṇṭumporuḷkāraṇamākappirital.ippiriviṉkaṇtalimakaṉnāṭṭikkaṭantum,kāṭṭikkaṭantumcellavēṇṭiyavaṉāvaṉ.ākiyālippiriviṟkukkālavelliiraṇṭutiṅkaḷām.
kaḷavuppuṇarccinikaḻumiṭam:-
kaḷavuppuṇarccipakaṟkuṟi.iravukkuṟieṉṉumiraṇṭupakutiyiṉiyuṭiyatu.pakaṟkuṟiyāvatuilliṉelliyikkaṭantaviṭattiltalivaṉtaliviyikkūṭutal.iravukkuṟiyāvatuilliṉelliyikkaṭavātaviṭattiltalivaṉtaliviyikkūṭutal.
kaḷavuveḷippaṭuvataṟkumuṉnikaḻumvaraviṟkuilakkaṇam:-
iyaṟkippuṇarccimutalākiyanāṉkuvakippaṭṭapuṇarcciyiṭattuntalimakaṉtāṉēteḷivupeṟṟuvaritalum,tōḻiyālteḷivuppaṭuttappaṭṭuvaritalumuṇṭu.
kaḷavuveḷippaṭṭapiṉvaritaṟkuilakkaṇam:
puṇarntuṭaṉpōyttalimakaṉtaṉṉūriṭattēvari
talum,mīṇṭuvantutammūriṉkaṇṇēvaritalum,uṭaṉpōkkuiṭiyīṭupaṭṭuvaritalum,talimakaḷiṉpeṟṟōrkaḷvaḻipaṭṭuvaritalumeṉakkaḷavoḻukkamveḷippaṭṭapiṉ
varitalnāṉkuvakippaṭum.ivaṟṟuḷmīṇṭumvaritaleṉpatutalimakaḷmaṉiyiṉkaṇṇēvaritalum,talimakaṉmaṉiyiṉkaṇṇēvaritalumeṉiraṇṭuvakippaṭum.
kaḷavuveḷippāṭṭiṟkuriyakiḷavittoki:
uṭaṉpōkku,kaṟpoṭupuṇarntakavvi,mīṭcieṉṟamūṉṟumkaḷavuveḷippāṭṭiṟkuriyakiḷavittokiyākum.
kaḷavoḻukkiṉkaṇpuṇarccinikaḻuntiṟam:
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇuḷḷattāṟpuṇarumpuṇarcciyum,uṭalālpuṇarumpuṇarcciyumeṉṟiruvakippuṇarccikaḷumtalimakaṉukkumtalimakaḷukkumuriyaṉavām.
kaḷiṟutarupuṇarcci:-
ituvumcevilikkuppāṅkiaṟattoṭuniṟkummuṟi
kaḷuḷoṉṟu.yāmcōliyilviḷiyāṭukiyāloruyāṉipāyavarumpōtuniṉmakaḷalaṟikkūvaavvaḻiyēvantāṉoruvēntaṉvirintuvantuniṉmakaḷiiṭap
pakkattilaṇittukkoṇṭuvalakkiyilvēleṭuttuyāṉiyoṭuporututurattiṉāṉ.aṉṟumutalavaḷavaṉniṉivākavēirukkiṟāḷeṉṟutaliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkatticcevilikkuppāṅkipulappaṭuttal.
kaṟpiṉilakkaṇam:-
talivaṉumtaliviyuṅkūṭinaṭattumilvāḻkkiyiṉkaṇuṇṭākiṉṟuḷḷamakiḻcciyum,ūṭalum,ūṭaluṇart
talum,pirivum,pirintaviṭattuuṇṭākiṉṟavikaṟpammutaliyaṉa
vellāmkaṟpiṉilakkaṇamākum.
kaṟpilnikaḻumpiriviṉvaki:-
talimakaṉparattimēṟkātalkoṇṭutaliviyippirintuparattiyarcērikkuccellumparattiyiṟpirivum,talivaṉkalvikāraṇamākappiriyumōtaṟpirivum,nāṭukāttaṟ
poruṭṭuppiriyumkāvaṟpirivum,aracariruvarpōrceytavaḻiavariccantuceyvippataṟkuppiriyumtūtiṟ
pirivum,taṉnaṇpaṉākiyavēntaṉukkuppakivēntarāliṭiyūṟunērntaviṭattuavviṭiyūṟṟinīkkutaṟporuṭṭuppiriyumtuṇivayiṟpirivum,poruḷīṭṭutalkāraṇamākappiriyumporuḷvayiṟpirivumeṉāṟuvakippirivumkaṟpukkālattunikaḻvaṉavām.
kaṟpiṟkuriyakiḷavittoki:
ilvāḻkki,parattiyiṟpirivu,ōtaṟpirivu,kāvaṟpirivu,tūtiṟpirivu,tuṇivayiṟpirivu,poruḷvayiṟpirivuākiyēḻumkaṟpiṟkuriyakiḷavittokikaḷākum.
kaṟpumaṇam:-
kaṟpeṉṟuciṟappittuccollappaṭuvatucaṭaṅkoṭuporuntik,koḷḷutaṟkuriyamuṟimiyiyuṭiyatalivaṉkoḷḷutaṟkuriyamuṟimiyiyuṭiyataliviyik,koṭuttaṟkuriyamuṟimiyiyuṭitaliviyiṉcuṟṟattār,teyvam,cāṉṟōrmaṇañceytukoṭuppamaṇantukoḷvatum,talivitalivaṉuṭaṉuṭaṉpōkkiṟceṉṟa
viṭattukkoṭuttaṟkuriyamuṟimiyiyuṭiyataliviyiṉtamarillātaviṭattumcaṭaṅkoṭukūṭiyamaṇamnaṭi
peṟuvatumkaṟpumaṇamākum.
kaṟpukkālattuttalivaṉpālnikaḻumnikaḻcci:
iruvakikkaṟpiṉkaṇṇuntalimakaṟkukkaḷaviṟpuṇarcciyum,vatuvippuṇarcciyumuṇṭueṉpatām.kātaṟparattiyar,kāmakkiḻattiyar,piṉmuṟivatuvipperuṅkulakkiḻattiyarākiyamūvakimakaḷirumannikaḻccikaḷukkuuriyamakaḷirāvar.ivarkaḷuḷ,kātaṟparattikaḷaviṟpuṇarccik
kuriyar.kāmakkiḻattiyum,piṉmuṟivatuvipperuṅkulakkiḻattiyumvatuvippuṇarccikkuriyamakaḷirāvar.
kaṟpoṭupuṇarntakavvi:-
ituvumkaḷavuveḷippāṭṭiṟkuriyakiḷavittoki
kaḷuḷoṉṟu.talivitalivaṉuṭiyuṭimiyāykkaṟpoṭukūṭiyiruttaliayalārviraviyacēriyiṉarpalarumaṟital.
kaṟpoṭupuṇarntakavviyiṉvaki:-
cevilipulampal,naṟṟāypulampal,kavarmaṉimaruṭci,kaṇṭōrirakkam,cevilipiṉtēṭiccēṟaleṉṟakaṟpoṭupuṇarntakavviaivakippaṭum.
kavvi-āravāram,oli.
kaṟpu:-
kaṟpikkappaṭutalāṟkaṟpāyiṟṟu.kaṟpittalāvatuaṟivum,oḻukkamum,talivaṉāṟkaṟpikkappaṭutalum,tāytantiyarālkaṟpikkappaṭutalum,cevilittāyālkaṟpikkappaṭutalum,cāṉṟōrālkaṟpikkappaṭutalumeṉakkaṟpittalpalavakippaṭum.
kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatucoṟṟiṟampāvi
kaṟpumaṉattiṇmi.
kaṟpiṉvaki:-
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉvaḻiyākavantakaṟpum,kaḷavoḻukkamiṉṟivantakaṟpumeṉakaṟpuiruvakippaṭum.
kaṟpiṟpuṇarcciyiṉvaki:-
kuravarkaḷāltirumaṇamceyvikkappeṟṟuppuṇarumpuṇarcciyum,talivaṉ,taliviyarkaḷuḷūṭalnērntakālattuavvūṭalvāykaḷālnīkkappeṟṟuppuṇarumpuṇarcciyumeṉakaṟpiṟpuṇarcciiruvakippaṭum.
kātaṟparattiyarilakkaṇam:-
yāvariyumvirumpātiyalpiyuṭiyacēripparattiyiṉmakaḷirāyttamaṉpiṉāltalimakaṉōṭupuṇarpavarkātaṟparattiyāvar.akkātaṟparattiyartalivaṉāltirumaṇamceytukōṭaṟkumuriyar.(parattiyarippēcānūltirukkuṟaḷ.)
kāmakkiḻattiyarilakkaṇam:-
cēripparattiyarpōlappalarkkumuriyāraṉṟioruvaṟkēurimipūṇṭuvaruṅkulapparattiyarmakaḷirāykkāmaṅkāraṇamākattalimakaṉāltirumaṇamceytukoḷḷappaṭṭavarkāmakkiḻattiyarāvar.
kārkālattiṉtaṉmi:-
vāṭikkāṟṟuvīcalum;cemmūtāyum(intirakōpamum)mayilum,(kēkayappaṟaviyum)tōṉṟimakiḻtalum;veṇ
kāntaḷ,ceṅkāntaḷ,koṉṟi,kūtāḷam,vēṅkimaram,kākkaṇañceṭi,mulli,kaṭampu,kāyāmutaliyaṉamalartalum;aṉṉamkiḷikuyilakavalum,kaṉṟuvarutalum,tāmarimalarnīrilmaṟitalumkārkkālattuttaṉmikaḷākum.
kāvaṟpiriviṉvaki:
talivaṉnāṭukāttaṟporuṭṭuppiriyumpirivukāvaṟpirivākum.appirivu,aṟappuṟaṅkāval,nāṭukāvaleṉiruvakippaṭum.aṟappuṟaṅkāvalāvatu.aṟattiṟkuviṭappaṭṭanilam,aṟaccālimutaliyavaṟṟikkāttalām.
kāvaṟpiriviṟkuuriyār:
aṟappuṟaṅkāvalaracarmutaliyaaṉivarkkumuriyatām.aracaṉum,aracaṉāṟciṟappuppeyarpeṟṟavarumnāṭukāvaṟpiriviṟkucciṟappākuriyarāvar.
kālamākiyamutaṟporuḷiṉvaki:-
poḻutueṉṉummutaṟporuḷ,perumpoḻutueṉṟumciṟupoḻutueṉṟumiraṇṭupākupāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyatām.
kiḻattimakiḻcci:-
taliviyiṉmakiḻcci.itutalivitāṉvaruntātiruntataṉkāraṇattippāṅkikkuurittalālviḷaṅkum.
kiḻavōṉmakiḻcci:-
talivaṉmakiḻcci-itutalimakaṉtaliviyiṉmuṉpāṅkiyippukaḻtalālviḷaṅkum.
kuṟiñcikkuriyaperumpoḻutumciṟupoḻutum:
kūtirkālamum,muṉpaṉikkālamum,yāmappoḻutumkuṟiñcittiṇikkuriyaperumpoḻutum,ciṟupoḻutumākum.
kuṟiñcikkarupporuḷ:-
teyvam-murukaṉ
talimiyar-poruppaṉ,veṟpaṉ,cilampaṉ,kuṟatti,koṭicci
makkaḷ-kuṟavar,kāṉavar,kuṟattiyar.
puḷ-kiḷi,mayil
vilaṅku-puli,karaṭi,yāṉi,ciṅkam.
ūr-ciṟukuṭi
nīr-aruvinīr,cuṉinīr
pū-vēṅkippū,kuṟiñcippū,kāntaṭpū.
maram-cantaṉam,tēkku,akil,acōku,nākam,mūṅkil
uṇavu-malinel,mūṅkilarici,tiṉi.
paṟi-toṇṭakappaṟi
yāḻ-kuṟiñciyāḻ
paṇ-kuṟiñcippaṇ
toḻil-veṟiyāṭal,malinelvitittal,tiṉi
kāttal,tēṉaḻitteṭuttal,kiḻaṅkukiṇṭieṭuttal,aruvinīrāṭal,cuṉinīrāṭal.
kuṟippaṟital:
talimakaḷuḷḷattunikaḻukiṉṟavēṭkiyiṉit(puṇaruṅkuṟippiṉi)talimakaṉukkuavaḷatukaṇkaḷēviḷaṅkaaṟivikkum.
eṭu:-
irunōkkivaḷuṇkaṇuḷḷatorunōkku
	nōynōkkoṉṟannōymaruntu.
kuṟiyuṟuṇartal:
itupāṅkimatiuṭaṉpāṭueṉṉumkaḷaviyalkiḷavittokikkuriyavakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.iyaṟkippuṇarccikkuppiṉṉētalimakaḷiṉuyirppāṅkiyiaṟintuavaḷivāyilākappeṟṟukkuṟiyuṟutalivaliyuṟuttattalimakaṉkaṇṇiyumtaḻiyumēnticcārntuniṉṟupāṅkiyiṉūrum,peyarumkeṭṭaporuḷiyumoḻintavumviṉāviyaviṭattuivaryāreṉavum,ivarmaṉattuḷatākiyeṇṇamyāteṉavum,ārāyntumavaṉuḷḷattueṇṇattitteḷintumkūṭṭamuṇmiyippāṅkiuṇartalkuṟiyuṟuṇartalākum.
kūtirpparuvattiṉtaṉmi:
aippaci,kārttikiākiyirutiṅkaḷumkūtirpparuva
mākum.ipparuvattilkūtirkkāṟṟuvīcum.kurukum,aṉṉamum,kokkum,caṅkum,naṇṭum,nattiyumeṉivimakiḻum.nīrteḷivākattōṉṟum.mīṉiṉamvaḷarcci
yaṭiyum.mēkamcūṟkoḷḷum.cantaṉam,ciṟucaṇpakam,cemparatti,nāṇalākiyavimalarum.īṇṭukkūṟātapaṟavikaḷum,vilaṅkukaḷummakkaṭṭokutiyuṭaṉvaruntum.iviyēkūtirpparuvattiṉtaṉmikaḷākum.
kikkiḷinikaḻuṅkālam:
kaḷaviṟpuṇarccikkumuṉkikkiḷinikaḻum.
kikkiḷi:
akapporuḷvakiēḻaṉuḷoṉṟu.orutalikkāmameṉappaṭṭuttalimakaṉkūṟṟāynikaḻvatu.kāṭci,aiyam,tuṇivu,kuṟippaṟitaleṉṉumnālvakiyāṉumkūṟṟunikaḻum.
kikkiḷiyilnikaḻumuripporuḷ:
talimakaḷippārttaviṭattuttalimakaṉukkuēṟpaṭṭamaṉanikaḻcciuḷḷanilimiyum,piṟakutaliviyiṉkuṟippuaṟiyumvariyilumnikaḻumnikaḻcciyum,taliviyietiriṭṭukkāṇutalumākiyanikaḻccikaḷkikkiḷiyilnikaḻumuripporuḷām.
kikkiḷiyiṉvaki:
kāṭci,aiyam,tuṇivu,kuṟippaṟitalākiyanāṉkumkik
kiḷittiṇiyiṉvakikaḷākum.
kikkiḷikkuriyār:
kuṟiñci,mulli,marutam,neytal,pālieṉṟukūṟap
paṭṭaivakinilattarumkikkiḷikkuuriyavarāvār.
ceppal;
itutalivaṉtaliviyaruṭaṉpōkkumīṭciyiṉ
vakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.muṉceṉṟōrtaliviyiṉvarukiyippāṅkiyarkkuccollutal.ceppal-collutal.
ceppal:
itutaṉmaṉivaritaliṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.tirumaṇamnaṭantamiyittōḻicevilikkukkūṟutalum,ataṉiccevilinaṟṟāykkukkūṟutalum,talimakaṉyāṉtaliviyimaṇantamiyinumcuṟṟattārkkukkūṟukeṉikuḷikkukkūṟaavaḷyāṉmuṉṉarēataṉiemmavarkkukkūṟiṉeṉeṉṟalumām.
ceviliurimi:
talimakaḷuṭiyanaṟṟāykkuttōḻiyākittalimakaṭkuvaruntuṉpaṅkaḷippōkki,nallaṟivumoḻukkamuṅkaṟpittuttalimakaḷivaḷarttatāycevilittāyāvāḷ.
cevilimakiḻcci:
cevilittāyiṉatumakiḻcci.itumaṇamaṉiceṉṟu
vantacevilinaṟṟāykkuttaliviyiṉkaṟpiṉtaṉmiyiyum,talivaṉtaliviyarkaḷiṉvāḻkkittaṉmiyiyum,avviruvarkaḷiṉkātaliyumuṇarttalālviḷaṅkum.
cevilipiṉtēṭiccellaliṉviri:
varuntukiṉṟanaṟṟāyiccevilitēṟumāṟukūṟutalum,taliviyiccevilitēṭippōkuṅkālattuvaḻiyiṭietir
varummukkōluṭiyaantaṇariviṉavutalumceviliviṉa
viyataṟkumukkōluṭiyaantaṇaratuulakiyaliyalpueṉṟukāraṇameṭuttukkāṭṭalum,cevilittāypālinilattuppeṇṇoṭupulampikkūṟutalum,cevilikuravueṉṉuṅkoṭiyoṭuvaruntutalum,cevilipālinilattiṉmēṟkālaḻuntiyakuṟiyikkaṇṭuiraṅkikkūṟutalum,uṭaṉpōkkuppōyttalivaṉumtaliviyumpōlaṉpukalantuvaḻiyiṭivaruvōriruvarikkaṇṭuavar
kaḷiṭamtaliviyippaṟṟiceviliviṉātalum,ceviliyiraṅkivaruntutalietirvantōrtēṟṟikkūṟutalum,cevilitaṉputalviyikkāṇātutuṉpammikutalumākiyoṉpatumcevilipiṉtēṭiccellaliṉvirikaḷākum.
cevilipulampaliṉviri:
taliviyikkāṇāviṭattuccevilittāypāṅkiyiviṉavutalum,talivitalivaṉuṭaṉuṭaṉpōkkucceṉṟatippāṅkicevilittāykkuaṟivittalum,taliviuṭaṉpōkkucceṉṟatippāṅkimūlamaṟintacevilivaruntikkūṟutalum,taliviuṭaṉpōkkuppōtaṟkuttaṉkuṟippiṉālaṟivittataṉmiyiaṟintilēṉeṉṟutaṉaṟiyāmiyinontukūṟutalum,uṭaṉpōkkucceṉṟatalivitaṉpālvaracceviliteyvamvāḻttalumākiyivicevilipulampaliṉvirikaḷākum.
cevilipiṉtēṭiccēṟal:
uṭaṉpōkkucceṉṟataliviyiccevivipiṉṉētēṭikkoṇṭucellutal.
cevilipulampal:
taṉpeṇkātalaṉuṭaṉceṉṟataṉiuṇarntucevilittāypulamputal.
ceviliaṟattoṭuniṟkuntiṟaṉ:
talimakaḷvēṟupāṭukaṇṭaviṭattuccevilittāyinaṟṟāyavvēṟupāṭṭiṟkukkāraṇamviṉāviyaviṭattuccevilinaṟṟāykkumuṉṉilippuṟamoḻiyālaṉṟimuṉṉilimoḻiyāṉtaliviyiṉkaḷaviveḷippaṭuttikkūṟuvāḷ.
ceviliaṟattoṭuniṟkuntiṟam:
ceviliaṟattoṭuniṟkuṅkālattunaṟṟāykkuttaliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkattiviḷaṅkaaṟivikkum.
celavaḻuṅkakkāraṇam:
vīṭṭiṉkaṇtaliviyiyumtaṉuḷḷattiṉiyumtēṟṟutaṟkākavum,iṭiccurattiṉiṭattuttaṉuḷḷat
tiṉittēṟṟutaṟkākavumpiriviṟkuvaruntuvāṉētaviratalimakaṉtāṉkuṟippiṭṭaceyalukkuppōkāmalirāṉ.(celavaḻuṅkal-piriviṟkuvaruntutal.)
ciṟupoḻutiṉvaki:
māli,yāmam,vikaṟi,viṭiyal,eṟpāṭu,naṇpakaleṉpaṉaciṟupoḻutiṉvakikaḷākum.
ivaṟṟuḷmāliyāvatu-irāppoḻutiṉmuṟpakutiyāmamāvatu-irāppoḻutiṉnaṭuppakuti,vikaṟiyāvatu-irāppoḻutiṉpiṟpakuti,viṭiyalāvatu-pakaṟpoḻutiṉmuṟpakuti,eṟpāṭāvatu-pakaṟpoḻutiṉpiṟpakuti,naṇpakalā
vatu-pakaliṉnaṭuppakuti.
talimakkaḷnāṭiṭiyiṭṭuccelluṅkālcelluntiṟaṉ:
ōtaṟpirivumutaliyapiriviṉkaṇtalimakkaḷnaṭantucellutalum,kutiri,yāṉimutaliyavaṟṟilūrntucellu
talum,kalattilamarntunīrvaḻiyākaccellutalumām.
talimakkaṭkākātakuṇam:
poṟāmi,aṟaṉaḻiyappiṟariccūḻumcūḻcci,tammipperiyarākamatittal,piṟaṉikkāṇātaviṭattuikaḻntukūṟutal,kaṭuñcolkūṟutal,maṟati,muyaṟciyiṉmi,tammuṭiyakulacciṟappiyeṇṇimatittuiṉpuṟutal,pētimi,tāṉkātalikkappaṭṭārikkaṇṭavaḻiavarpōlvārivareṉoppiṭṭuniṉittalākiyavitalimakkaṭkuiruttalkūṭātakuṇaṅkaḷākum.
talimakaḷaṟattoṭuniṟkumiṭam:
iyaṟkippuṇarcci,iṭantalippāṭu,pāṅkaṟkūṭṭameṉṟamūṉṟupuṇarccikkaṇṇum,oruvaḻittaṇattalāṉumtalivaṉvaraviṭivittupporuḷvayiṟpiritalāṉum,cevilitalimakaṉippakaṟkuṟi,iravukkuṟiākiyiṭattilkāṇṭalāṉum,iṟceṟittalāṉum,taṉakkuttuṉpammikumāyiṉtaṉṉittōḻiviṉaviyaviṭattumviṉavāviṭattumtalimakaḷtōḻikkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ.
talimakaḷāṟpeṟumiyaṟkippuṇarcci:
talimakaḷāṟpeṟumiyaṟkippuṇarccitalimakaṉmuyaṟciyālmuṭivatākumippuṇarccivēṭkiyuṇarttal,maṟuttal,uṭaṉpaṭal,kūṭṭameṉṟanāṉkuvakiyiṉiyuṭiyatu.
ivaṟṟuḷvēṭkiuṇarttalāvatu-talivikkuttali
vaṉtaṉvēṭkiyitterivittal,maṟuttalāvatu-taliviataṉimaṟuttal,uṭaṉpaṭalāvatu-talivipuṇarccikkuiṇaṅkutal,kūṭutalāvatu-talivaṉtaliviyikkūṭutal.
talimakaḷāṟpeṟumiyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉviri:
irantupiṉṉiṟṟaṟkeṇṇalirantupiṉṉiṟṟal,muṉ
ṉiliyākkal,meytoṭṭuppayiṟal,poypārāṭṭal,iṭampeṟṟuttaḻāal,vaḻipāṭumaṟuttal,iṭiyūṟukiḷattal,nīṭuniṉintiraṅkal,maṟuttetirkōṭal,vaṟitunakitōṟṟal,muṟuvaṟkuṟippuṇarttal,muyaṅkutaluṟuttal,puṇarcciyiṉmakiḻtal,pukaḻtal,mutaliyapatiṉintumtalimakaḷāṟpuṇarumiyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉvirikaḷākum.
talivikkuūṭalnikaḻumiṭam:
ayaṉmaṉippirivu,ayaṟcēriyiṉakaṟci,puṟanakarppōkkuākiyamūvakipparattiyiṟpiriviṉkaṇṇumtalivaṉmītutaliviūṭalkoḷvāḷ.(ūṭal-kaṇavaṉmaṉiviyarukkiṭiyilēṟpaṭumkātaṟpiṇakku)
taliviaṟattoṭuniṟkuntiṟaṉ:
kaḷavuppuṇarcciyālalareḻutalkaṇṭutantiyarmutalāyiṉōrveṟuppaṭintataṟintatalivaṉpirintucellapiṟivāṟṟātataliviaḻa,tōḻiarukiliruntuāṟṟuvittuavaḷkaṇṇīrittuṭittuaḻutaṟkukkāraṇaṅkēṭṭalum,talimakaḷtaṉaḻukikkukkāraṇaṅkūṟutalum,talivaṉteyvattikkāṭṭikkariyeṉṟucūḷuṟavucolla,uṇmiyeṉṟuteḷintuataṉippāṅkikkupporuntakkūṟutalum,talivitalivaṉnīṅkiṉamikūṟalum,talimakaṉpaṇpittōḻipaḻittukkūṟutalum,aṅṅaṉaṅkūṟakkēṭṭatalimakaḷcoṟporuḷōriyalpupaṭamoḻitalum,teyvattiṉmuṉṉēpiriyēṉeṉṟuāṇiyiṭṭuppirintupōṉārālteyvaṅkoṭunteyvamātalālcīṟātapaṭiavareṅkaṭkukkuṟṟañceytārallar,nīpoṟuttukkoḷeṉṟuvēṇṭikkoḷḷutaṟkuiraṇṭupērumcelvōmeṉṟutalivipāṅkiyuṭaṉkūṟutalum,taṉmeyvēṟu
pāṭṭālum,ūriluḷḷāralartūṟṟalālumnaṟṟāyuḷḷattilveṟuppuuṇṭākieṉṉikkāvalceytāḷeṉṟutalivipāṅkikkukkūṟutalum,cevilittāyiruṭkuṟiyiṭattuttalivaṉvarakkaṇṭāḷeṉattalivipāṅkikkukkūṟutalumākiyēḻumpāṅkikkuttaliviaṟattoṭuniṟkuntiṟaṉākum.
talimakaṉpiriyumpiriviṟkucciṟappuviti:
talimakaṉukkukkūṟappaṭṭaaṟuvakippirivēyaṉṟi,kuṟiñci,mulli,marutamneytalākiyanālvakinilattiṉumteyvaṅkaṭkunaṭipeṟumtiruviḻākkāraṇamākavum,mēṟkūṟiyanilattilvāḻummakkaḷaṟaneṟitavaṟiyaviṭattuavaṟṟinīkkiaṟattiniliniṟuttaṟporuṭṭum,talimakaṉtaliviyippirintucelvāṉ.
talimakaṉpirivukkuilakkaṇam:
ōtaṟpirivu,kāvaṟpirivu,tūtiṟpirivu,tuṇivayiṟpirivu,poruḷvayiṟpirivuākiyaivakipirivukkuttalimakaṉpiriyumpoḻututaliviyiṭamtampiriviccolliyumpirivāṉ,collāmalumpirivāṉ.āṉālpāṅkiyiṭattuttaṉṉuṭiyapirivikkūṟiviṭuvāṉ.talivaṉtaliviyiṭamtampirivikkūṟāviṭṭālumavaṉatukuṟippāltalivaṉpiriyappōkiṟāṉeṉpatittaliviaṟintukoḷvāḷ.
talimakaṉcelavaḻuṅkal:
ōtaṟpirivumutaliyaivakippiriviṉkaṇṇumpiriyun
talimakaṉpōtaṟkēyaṉṟippōkāmikkumuriyavaṉāvāṉ.(aḻuṅkal=kavaluṟal;cellattayaṅkutal.)
taṉmaṉivarital:
uṭaṉpōkkuppōymīṇṭuvantatalivaṉtaṉṉūrkkuttaliviyikkoṇṭuceṉṟutaṉmaṉiyiṉkaṇvarintukoḷḷutal.ituviṉātal,ceppal,mēvutaleṉamūvakippaṭum.
taṉmaṉivaritaliṉviri:
talivinaṟṟāytaṉillilmaṇañceyyumviruppiṉāṟceviliyiviṉātalum,cevilittāykkuttōḻitalivaṉtaliviyimaṇantukoṇṭamiyitterivittalum,taliviyiṉmaṇamnaṭantēṟiyaticcevilinaṟṟāykkukkūṟutalum,talivaṉtōḻikkuyāṉtaliviyivarintukoṇṭatinumarkkukkūṟukaveṉakkūṟutalum,tōḻiicceytiyimuṉṉamēkūṟiyatittalivaṉukkukkūṟutalumākiyaintumtaṉmaṉivaritaliṉvirikaḷākum.
tiṇimayakkam:
orutiṇikkuriyanilamoḻintamutaṟporuḷum,karup
poruḷummaṟṟorutiṇikkuriyamutaṟporuḷ,karup
poruḷōṭucērntuvaruvatutiṇimayakkamām.
tuṇivayiṟpirivukkuuriyar:
taṉnaṇpaṉākiyaaracaṉukkuppakivēntarkaḷāltuṉpamnērntaviṭattu,attuṉpattippōkkattuṇiyākattalimakaṉpiriyumpirivutuṇivayiṟpirivākum.ippirivuōrāṇṭukālavelliyiṉiyuṭiyatām.ippiriviṉkaṇpirintuceṉṟatalimakaṉumviṉinīṭṭittaviṭattuppācaṟikkaṇṇēyiruntutaliviyiyeṇṇippulamputalumuṇṭu.
tūtiṟpirivu:
aracariruvartammuḷpōrceytavaḻiappōriniṟuttiavariccantuceyvippataṟkuttalivaṉpiriyumpirivutūtiṟpirivākum.ippirivuōrāṇṭukālavelliyiṉi
yuṭiyatu.ippiriviṉkaṇceṉṟatalimakaṉviṉinīṭṭit
taviṭattuppācaṟikkaṇṇēyiruntutaliviyiyeṇṇippulamputalumuṇṭu.
teḷittal:
itumīṭciyiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.cevilittāynaṟ
ṟāykkuttalivineṭuntūramceṉṟuviṭṭataṉikkūṟiavaḷitteḷivittalum,uṭaṉpōymīṇṭuvarukiyiltalivaṉtaliviyiṉatuūriccērntamiyikkūṟittaliviyitteḷivittalumām.
teyvattālpuṇarumiyaṟkippuṇarcci:
teyvattāṟpuṇarumiyaṟkippuṇarccimuyaṟciyiṉṟittāṉēmuṭivatākum.ippuṇarccikalantuḻimakiḻtal,taliviyiṉaḻakiṉippārāṭṭikkūṟutal,ēṟpuṟavaṇitaleṉṉummūvakiviriyiṉiyuṭiyatām.
teḷivu:
talivaṉkūṟiyacollittalivimeyeṉatteḷintuāṟṟutalteḷivākum.
tēṟṟal:
ituvumuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.kaṇṭōrtalivaṉpatiaṇimiyāṉamiyikkūṟiviṭuppattaliva
ṉuṭaṉceṉṟatalivikkuttaṉpatiaṭintataṉikkūṟittalivaṉavaḷittēṟacceytal.
tōḻiurimi:
cevilikkumakaḷāyppiṟantunaṉmiyuntīmiyumārāyumaṟivuṭiyavaḷāyttalimakaṭkucciṟantatuṇiyāy,avaḷatuvaruttattippōkkuvataṟkuriyaaṉpuporuntiyatuṇiyākiruppavaḷētōḻiyāvaḷ.
naki:
itueṇvakimeyppāṭukaḷuḷoṉṟu.ikaḻtal,iḷami,aṟiviṉmi,maṭamieṉṟukūṟappaṭṭanāṉkiṭattumnakitōṉṟum.ivaṟṟuḷikaḻtaleṉpatutāṉpiṟariikaḻntunakutalum,piṟarālikaḻappaṭṭavaḻittāṉnakutalumeṉiraṇṭāmiḷamiyeṉpatutāṉiḷamiyāṟpiṟarinakutalum,piṟariḷamikaṇṭutāṉnakutalumeṉiraṇṭāmpētamiyeṉpatuaṟiviṉmimaṭamiyeṉpatuaṟintumaṟiyātatupōṉṟuiruttal.
(e.ṭu)
nakutakkaṉarēnāṭumīkkūṟunar(puṟam-12)
	itueḷḷalkāraṇamākanakipiṟantatu.
	nakiyākiṉṟētōḻi.........mammarneñciṉaṉ			toḻutuniṉṟatuvē	(akam-56)
itupētamikāraṇamākanakipiṟantatu.
naṟṟāyaṟattoṭuniṟkuntiṟam:
naṟṟāyaṟattoṭuniṟkuṅkālattuttaliviyiṉkaḷa
voḻukkattivāytiṟantukūṟāḷ.ataṉittalimakaḷiṉtantiyumuṭaṉpiṟantārumnaṟṟāyiṉkuṟippiṉāṉēaṟintukoḷvar.
naṟṟāypulampal:
taṉṉuṭiyapeṇkātaluṭaṉceṉṟataṉiuṇarntunaṟṟāypulamputal.
naṟṟāypulampaliṉviri:
cevilittāynaṟṟāykkuttaliviyiṉuṭaṉpōkkiveḷippaṭuttikkūṟutalum,taliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkattiaṟintanaṟṟāypāṅkiyiṭamnīitimuṉṉamēaṟivit
tiruntālattalivaṉukkuavaḷimaṇañceytukoṭut
tiruppaṉēeṉṟukūṟippāṅkiyoṭuvaruntutalum,avvāṟukūṟakkēṭṭapāṅkivaruntutalumpāṅkiyiṉvaruttattikkaṇṭanaṟṟāyvaruntutalum.naṟṟāypāṅkiyoṭuvaruntutalum,naṟṟāyayalāroṭuvaruntutalum,naṟṟāytalivipaḻakiviḷiyāṭumiṭaṅkaḷōṭunontukūṟutalum,ākiyiviyellāmnaṟṟāypulampaliṉvirikaḷākum.
nilamākiyamutaṟporuḷiṉvaki:
maliyummalicārntiṭamum,curamumcurañcārntiṭamum,kāṭumkāṭucārntiṭamum,vayalumvayalcārntiṭamum,kaṭalumkaṭalcārntiṭamumeṉamutaṟporuḷpattuvakippaṭum.
nīkkam:
ituvumuṭaṉpōkkiṭiyīṭṭiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.talimakaṉtalimakaḷiṉcuṟṟattārpiṉvarutaliuṇarntutalimakaḷiviṭuttuccellutal.
neytaṟkarupporuḷ:
teyvam	-	varuṇaṉ
uyarntōr	-	cērppaṉ,pulampaṉ,paratti,					nuḷicci
kuṭikaḷ	-	nuḷiyar,nuḷicciyar,					paratar,parattiyar,						aḷavar,aḷattiyar
puḷ	-	kaṭaṟkākam
vilaṅku	-	cuṟāmīṉ
ūr	-	pākkam,paṭṭiṉam
nīr	-	uvarkkēṇi,uvarnīr
pū	-	neytaṟpūtāḻampū,muṇṭakappū,				aṭampampū
maram	-	kaṇṭal,puṉṉi,ñāḻal
uṇavu	-	mīṉumuppumviṟṟuppeṟṟaporuḷ
paṟi	-	mīṉkōṭpaṟi,nāvāyppampi
yāḻ	-	viḷariyāḻ
paṇ	-	cevvaḻippaṇ
toḻil	-	mīṉpiṭittal,uppuṇṭākkal,avi			viṟṟal,mīṉuṇakkal,avaṟṟi				uṇṇavarumpaṟavikaḷiōṭṭutal,			kaṭalāṭal.
neytaṟkuriyaperumpoḻutumciṟupoḻutum:
kārkālam,kūtirkālam,muṉpaṉikkālam,piṉpaṉikkālam,iḷavēṉiṟkālam,mutuvēṉiṟkālameṉṟaaṟuvakipperumpoḻutum,eṟpāṭākiyaciṟupoḻutumneytaltiṇik
kuriyavaṉavām.
pakaṟkuṟi:
talivaṉtaliviyippakaṟkuṟiyiṭattukkūṭutalpakaṟ
kuṟiyākum.kuṟi=kuṟippiṭṭiṭam.atukūṭṭal,kūṭal,pāṅkiṟkūṭṭal,vēṭṭaleṉanālvakippaṭum.pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭattiṟkuriyakiḷavikaḷuḷiṟutiyiṟpāṅkitalimakaṉukkukkuṟiyiṭaṅkūṟalmutalākattalivaṉviruntiṉivirumputalīṟākakkūṟappaṭṭapaṉṉiraṇṭukiḷavikaḷumitaṟkuriyakiḷavikaḷākum.
pakaṟkuṟiiṭiyīṭu:
pakaṟkuṟiyiṭattuvantatalimakaṉkuṟikkaṭcellātuiṭiyīṭupaṭṭuppōtalpakaṟkuṟiiṭiyīṭākum.
pakaṟkuṟiiṭiyīṭṭiṉvaki:
vilakkal,cēṟal,kalakkameṉamūṉṟuvakiyiṉiyuṭiya
tākumpakaṟkuṟiiṭiyīṭu.vilakkal-talivaṉumtaliviyumkuṟiyiṭattuvarutalivilakkal.cēṟal-tōḻi,taliviyiāṭumiṭattiṉiṉṟuaḻittuccellutal,kalakkam-talivaṉiaṭiyappeṟāmiyāltalivimaṉaṅkalaṅkutalum,taliviyiaṭiyappeṟāmiyāltalivaṉmaṉaṅkalaṅkutalumām.
pakaṟkuṟiiṭiyīṭṭiṉviri:
talivaṉippāṅkikuṟiyiṭattuvaruvativilakkal.taliviyippāṅkikuṟiyiṭattuvaruvativilakkal.talivitāṉviḷiyāṭiyiṭattippārttuvaruntutal.pāṅkiviḷiyāṭumiṭamviṭṭunīṅkittaliviyikkoṇṭutammūrkkuccellutal.talivaṉkuṟiyiṭamākiyamātavippantariṭattuvantunīṭṭittuniṉintiraṅkutal.talivaṉtaliviyillātatiṉippuṉattinōkkivaruntutal.taliviyiṉūrtēṭiccelleṇṇimayaṅkiyaniliyiltalivaṉkūṟutalākiyēḻumpakaṟkuṟiiṭiyīṭṭiṉvirikaḷākum.
paṭikkalappayiṟcikkuriyār:
paṭikkalaṉkaḷikkaṟṟalum,yāṉiyum,tērum,kutiri
yummutalāyiṉūrtalumaracar,vaṇikar,vēḷāḷarākiyamūvarkkumuriyaṉavām.
parattiyiṟpirivu:
talimakaṉparattimēṟkātalkoṇṭutaliviyippirintuparattiyarcērikkuccellutalparattiyiṟpirivākum.
parattiyiṟpiriviṉvaki:
ayalmaṉikkuttaliviyippirintucellutal,ayaṟ
cērikkuttaliviyippirintucellutal,nakarppuṟattiṟkuttaliviyippirintucellutaleṉapparattiyiṟpirivumūṉṟuvakippaṭum.ivaṟṟuḷayalmaṉippirivukāmak
kiḻattiyarkāraṇamākappirivāṉ.iraṇṭāvatumaṇañceytukoḷḷappaṭṭapetumpipparuvakkiḻattiyum,kātaṟ
parattiyumviḻavumkāraṇamākattalimakaṉayaṟcēriyiṉkaṇṇēpirivāṉ.iḷañcōliyilviḷiyāṭutalkāraṇa
mākavum,nīrviḷiyāṭṭuviḷiyāṭutalkāraṇamākavumputiyaḷākiyaparattiyittērmēlēṟṟikkoṇṭunakarppuṟattiṟkuppōvāṉ.
parattiyiṟpirivunikaḻtaṟkākakkālam:
pūppunikaḻntu(vīṭṭuvilakku)nīrāṭiyapiṉpaṉṉiraṇṭunāḷuṅkaruttarikkuṅkālamātalāṟparattiyiṟpirintatalimakaṉappaṉṉiraṇṭunāḷuntalimakaḷippiriyappeṟāṉ.
pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam:(pāṅkaṉ=tōḻaṉpāṅku=pakkam)
pāṅkaṉāṟkūṭuṅkūṭṭampāṅkaṟkūṭṭamākum.itucārtal,kēṭṭal,cāṟṟal,etirmaṟi,nērtal,kūṭal,pāṅkiṟkūṭṭaleṉēḻuvakippaṭum.ivaṟṟuḷcārtalāvatu-talivaṉtaṉpāṅkaṉiccērtal.kēṭṭalāvatu-pāṅkaṉtalivaṉatuvāṭṭattikkaṇṭuuṉakkuyātuṟṟatueṉṟutalivaṉiviṉātal.cāṟṟalāvatu-talivaṉvāṭṭattiṉkāraṇattitterivittal.etirmaṟiyāvatu-pāṅkaṉniṉakkitutakātueṉattalivaṉiiṭitturittal.tērtalāvatu-talivaṉcoṟpaṭitaliviyicceṉṟupārttuvantapāṅkaṉtalivaṉkaruttiṟkuicital.kūṭalāvatu-talivaṉceṉṟutaliviyikkūṭal.pāṅkiṟkūṭṭalāvatu-niṉuyirppāṅkiyōṭuvarukeṉakkūṟittalivaṉtaliviyiavaḷpāṅkiyarpāṟcellaviṭuttal.
pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattiṉviri:
taliviyāluṇṭākiyivvēṭkipāṅkaṉālaṉṟittīrāteṉṟeṇṇittalivaṉpāṅkiṉiccārtalumpāṅkaṉtalivaṉatuuḷḷamumtōḷumvāṭiyavēṟupāṭṭippārttuniṉatuivvēṟupāṭuēṟpaṭakkāraṇamyātuveṉṟukēṭṭalum,ivvāṟukēṭṭapāṅkaṉukkuttalimakaṉtaṉakkuēṟpaṭṭavēṟupāṭṭiṉkāraṇattiviḷampalum,talivaṉkūṟiyakāraṇattikkēṭṭakaṟṟaṟipāṅkaṉiṭittukkūṟutalum.pāṅkaṉkūṟiyavaṟṟikkēṭṭatalivaṉavaṉatukaṭṭuriyimaṟuttukkūṟutalum,avvāṟukūṟiyatalivaṉippāṅkaṉpaḻittukkūṟutalum,talivaṉtaṉvēṭkiyiṉtāṅkamuṭiyātaniliyikkūṟutalum,pāṅkaṉtalivaṉiṉniliyiyeṇṇittaṉṉuḷḷattēyiraṅkutalum,pāṅkaṉtalivaṉnilikaṇṭuvaruntiavaṉōṭukūṟutalum,taliviyippuṇarāviṭiltalivaṉiṟantupaṭuvāṉeṉṟuaṟintu,talivaṉinōkkiniṉṉālkāṇappaṭṭatalivievviṭattuniṟpāḷ?avaḷatutaṉmiyeṉṉeṉṟupāṅkaṉviṉātalum.talivaṉtaṉṉāṟkāṇappaṭṭataliviniṟkumiṭam,avaḷatutaṉmiākiyavaṟṟikkūṟutalum.nīkūṟiyakuṟiyiṭattucceṉṟutaliviyikkaṇṭuvarukiṟēṉeṉṟutalivaṉittēṟṟutalum,talivaṉkūṟiyakuṟiyiṭattuttaliviyikkāṇappāṅkaṉcellutalum,pāṅkaṉavviṭattuttaliviyippārttalum,taliviyiṉpēraḻakikkaṇṭupāṅkaṉkāṇāmuṉṉamentalivaṉiaṟiviṉṟiikaḻntaṉemeṉṟuiraṅkikkūṟutalum,taliviyiṉkaṇvaliyiṟcikkiattaṭiyōṭuntalivaṉnammiṭattuvantatuviyappeṉṟupāṅkaṉaticayittukkūṟutalum,taliviyippāṅkaṉviyantukūṟutalum,talivikuṟiyiṭattuttaṉittuniṟkiṉṟaniliyikkaṇṭuvantupāṅkaṉtalivaṟkukkūṟutalum,pāṅkaṉkaṭṭaḷippaṭitalivaṉkuṟiyiṭattuccellutalum,talivaṉtaliviyippārttalum,taliviyippuṇarntumakiḻtalum,taliviyippukaḻtalum.iṉinīvaruṅkālniṉuyirppāṅkiyōṭuvarukeṉattaliviyinōkkit
talivaṉkūṟutalum,talivaṉtaliviyiāyattucceluttutalumākiyirupattēḻutuṟikaḷumpāṅkaṟkūṭṭattiṉvirikaḷākum.
pāṅkimatiuṭaṉpāṭu:
matiuṭaṉpāṭu:aṟivālopputal.pāṅkitaliviyiṉvēṟupāṭukaṇṭupuṇarcciuṇmiaṟintuārāyntutaṉmatiyiuṭaṉpaṭuttalpāṅkimatiuṭaṉpāṭākum.itumuṉṉuṟavuṇartal,kuṟiyuṟavuṇartal,iruvarumuḷvaḻiavaṉvaravuṇartaleṉamūvakippaṭum.
pāṅkiaṟattoṭuniṟkumtiṟam:
muṉṉilippuṟamoḻiyum,muṉṉilimoḻiyumākiyiraṇṭiṉālumpāṅkicevilittāykkuaṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ.muṉṉilippuṟamoḻiyāvatumuṉṉiṟpārkkukkūṟavēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟippiṟarkkukkūṟuvatupōlkūṟutal.muṉṉilimoḻiyāvatumuṉṉiṟpārkkunērēkūṟutal.
pāṅkiaṟattoṭuniṟkumnili:
cevilittāytalimakaḷvēṟṟumikkukkāraṇaṅkēṭṭaviṭattum,cevilittāyveṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉiyaḻittumakaṭkunōyvantavaḻiyum,atunīṅkumvaḻiyumcollavēṇṭumeṉṟukēṭṭaviṭattuteyvamvantāṭumpōtupāṅkiatteyvattiāṭavēṇṭāeṉṟuvilakkutalum,avvāṟuveṟivilakkiyataṟkuppāṅkiyiccevilikāraṇamviṉāviyaviṭattum,pāṅkipūttarupuṇarcci,puṉaltarupuṇarcci,kaḷiṟutarupuṇarcciākiyavaṟṟāltaliviyiṉkaḷaviccevilittāykkuveḷippaṭuttuvāḷ.
pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭattiṉviri:
talivaṉtaṉuḷḷattiṉkaṇtalivimēlkoṇṭakātalippāṅkiyiṭaṅkūṟutal.ivvāṟutalivaṉkūṟiya
taṟkuppāṅkikulamuṟiporuntātueṉamaṟuttukkūṟutal,talivaṉtaliviyiṉkulattiuyarttikkūṟutal.talivaṉtaliviyiṭattukkātalkoṇṭatuaṟiyāḷpōṉṟunīevaḷiṭattukkātalkoṇṭāyeṉappāṅkiviṉātal,talivaṉtaliviyiṉiyalpukaḷiiyampal.taliviteyvappeṇeṉṟukūṟiavaḷiaṭivatuarumiyeṉattalivaṉukkuttaliviyatuarumiyippāṅkikūṟutal.talivaṉtaliviiṉṟiyamiyāmikūṟal.niṉkuṟiyinīyēceṉṟutaliviyiṭaṅkūṟukaveṉattalivaṉiṭampāṅkikūṟutal.pāṅkiyittalivaṉpaḻittal.talivipiṟartuyaramaṟiyāḷeṉappētimimippāṅkitalivaṉuḷaṅ
koḷakkūṟal,talivitaṉakkaruḷiṉataṉmiyiyum.avaḷatumūtaṟivuṭimiyiyumtalivaṉpāṅkiyiṭaṅkūṟal.ivvāṟukūṟakkēṭṭapāṅkimuṉniṉvēṭkitīrttāḷeṉṟukūṟiṉāyē,avvāṟēiṉṉuṅkūṭiniṉvēṭkiyittīrttukkoḷkaveṉappāṅkikūṟutal.ivvāṟukūṟakkēṭṭatalivaṉvēṭkinōytaṉṉivaruttun
taṉmiyippāṅkiyiṭamkūṟutal.ivvāṟukūṟakkēṭṭapāṅkiulakilvēṭkikoṇṭōrcāṉṟōrimuṉṉiṭṭuvarintukoḷvar:avvāṟēnīyuntaliviyivarintukoḷkeṉakkūṟutal.ataṉittalimakaṉmaṟuttal,eṅkaḷuṭiyatamarpuṉattiirākkāvalkākkavaruvar.avarniṉṉikkāṇilpollātavarāvarākiyālnīippuṉamviṭṭunīṅkukeṉaaccuṟuttukkūṟutal,talivaṉtāṉkoṇṭu
vantataḻiyuṅkaṇṇiyumākiyakiyuṟipporuḷippukaḻntukūṟutal.pāṅkiataṉimaṟuttal,āṟṟāmiyākiyaneñciṉōṭutalivaṉvaruntikkūṟutal.ittuṇiyumintāmnāḷnaṭipeṟumnikaḻccikaḷākum.
maṟunāṭkāliyilvantatalimakaṉpāṅkiyālkāriyamnaṭipeṟātueṉṟeṇṇiiṉināmmaṭalēṟutalēceyyavēṇṭiyaceyaleṉattaṉuḷḷattilkūṟikkoḷḷutal,maṭalēṟṟiṉiulakiṉmēlvittuppāṅkikkukkūṟutal,ammaṭalēṟṟiṉittalivaṉtaṉmēlvittukkūṟutal.ivvāṟukūṟakkēṭṭapāṅkikiḻitīṭṭiyapiṟakallavāmaṭalēṟuvatu,ākiyālemmuṭiyataliviyatuuṟuppukkaḷtīṭṭutaṟkuarumiyuṭiyaṉeṉṟukūṟutal.ivvāṟutaliviyiṉuṟuppukkaḷeḻutuvatuariteṉṟukūṟiyapāṅkiyinōkki,yāṉtaliviyiṉuṟuppukaḷieḻutavallēṉeṉṟutalivaṉtaṉṉittāṉēpukaḻntukūṟutal.avvāṟutalivaṉkūṟakkēṭṭapāṅkimaṭalēṟattakātueṉaaruṇmuṟimikūṟutal.talivaṉuyirittāṅkikkoṇṭuniṟkumnilimiyākiyamoḻiyippāṅkikūṟutal.
talivikāmiṉpamnukartaṟkuriyaparuvamuṭiyaḷallaḷeṉattaliviyatuiḷamittaṉmiyippāṅkitalivaṟkukkūṟutal,talivitaṉṉittuṉpuṟuttiyatiṟattippāṅkikkuttalivaṉkūṟutal.taliviyiṉkālapparuvattiṉarumiyippāṅkikūṟutal,ivvāṟukūṟakkēṭṭatalivaṉmaliyakattuyāṉvantataṉmiyikkūṟuvāyāṉālavaryāvareṉṟukūṟātuniṉṉittaḻuvikkoḷvāḷeṉattaliviyiṉcevviyeḷimiyippāṅkikkūṟutal.nīviriruvarumottueṉṉimaṟittapiṉikkaḷavoḻukkamoḻukutaṟkueḷiteṉanakiyāṭikkūṟutal.pāṅkinakiyāṭikkūṟutalpoṟāmaltalivaṉpulantukūṟutal.ivvāṟuvaruntikkūṟiyatalivaṉippāṅkitēṟṟutal.talivaṉkoṭuttataḻiyuṅkaṇṇiyumākiyakiyuṟipporuḷip
pāṅkiēṟṟukkoḷḷutal.pāṅkikiyuṟiyēṟṟapiṉputalivaṉtuṉpamnīṅkikkūṟutal.
talivaṉatutuṉpattippōkkoppukkoṇṭapāṅkitaliviyiṭattuttalivaṉatukuṟiyiyuṇarttal.pāṅkiivvurikūṟavētalivitāṉkēṭṭuaṟiyāḷpōlamaṉattiṟkarutātavēṟoṉṟikkarutikkūṟutal.taliviyiṉniṉivākavantavaṉikkaṇṭatippāṅkitalivikkukkūṟutal.taṉkaḷavoḻukkattippāṅkiyiṭamtalivimaṟittal.ivvāṟumaṟittukkūṟiyataliviyiuṉakkunāṉvēṟōeṉṟuuvakiyāyttaḻuvikkoṇṭukūṟutal.pāṅkitalivaṉtantakiyuṟiyippukaḻntukūṟutal.
talivaṉvēṭkiyālkoṇṭatuṉpaniliyittalivikkuppāṅkikūṟutal.ivvāṟupāṅkikūṟiyataṟkuttaliviviṭikūṟātatāliṉiavarvariṉeṉṉālmaṟuttaṟkuiyalāteṉappāṅkiporuttikkūṟutal.talivaṉeṇṇamum,kuṟippumnammiṭamirappavaṉpōṟkāṇappaṭavilli,vēṟueṇṇamuṭiyavaṉākattōṉṟiyateṉṟukūṟutal.tōḻitaliviyicciṉattal.talivipāṅkiyicciṉattal.iṟutiyākattalivipāṅkikiyiluḷḷatalivaṉatukiyuṟiyiēṟṟukkoḷḷutal.
talivitalivaṉtantakiyuṟipporuḷākiyamāntaḻiyiēṟṟukkoṇṭatittalivaṉukkukkūṟutal.pāṅkitalimakaṉukkukkuṟiyiṭaṅkūṟutal.pāṅkitaliviyikkuṟiyiṭattukkoṇṭupōtal.pāṅkitalimakaḷikkuṟiyiṭattuyttunīṅkutal,talivitalivaṉiṭattuetirppaṭutal.puṇarntumakiḻtal.puṇarccikkuppiṉtalivaṉtaliviyippukaḻtal.talimakaṉtalimakaḷitōḻiyarkūṭṭattiṟcella
viṭuttal.taliviyikkuṟiyiṭattuniṟuttippōyiṉapāṅkitalivaṉpōyiṉapiṉputāṉkiyuṟikkuppōyiṉapāvaṉiyāykkiyuṟikoṇṭuvantukāṭṭal.taliviyippāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭal.talimakaḷiāyakkūṭṭattilcērttuviṭṭumaṟupaṭiyumtirumpivantutalimakaṉtaliviyimaṟavāmivēṇṭumeṉṟutalivaṉiṭampāṅkikūṟutal.uṟaviṉarayalūriliruntuvantālavarkkuuṇavukoṭuttuōmpuvatuulakiyalpākum.avvulakiyalmuṟippaṭitalimakaṉiemmūrkkuvantuiruntupōṅkaḷeṉappakaṟkuṟiyippāṅkivilakkutal.avvuṇaviṉittalivaṉvirumputalākiyaaṟupattorukiḷavikaḷumpāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭattiṉvirikaḷākum.
pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭam:
pāṅkikūṭṭivikkattalivaṉtaliviyikkūṭutalpāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭamākum.ikkūṭṭamirantupiṉṉiṟṟal,cēṭpaṭi,maṭaṟkūṟṟu,maṭalvilakku,uṭaṉpaṭal,maṭaṟkūṟṟoḻital,kuṟinayappittal,nayattal,kūṭṭal,kūṭal,āyaṅkūṭṭal,vēṭṭaleṉappaṉṉiraṇṭuvakippaṭum.
irantupiṉṉiṟṟal-talivaṉpāṅkiyiṉiṭattuttaṉkuṟiyittīrkkavēṇṭumeṉitamākaccollutal.cēṭ
paṭi-pāṅkitalivaṉkuṟiyittīrkkuṭaṉpaṭamaṟuttal.maṭaṟkūṟṟu-talivaṉmaṭalēṟuvaleṉakkūṟutal.maṭalvilakku-pāṅki,avaṉmaṭalēṟutalivilakkal.uṭaṉpaṭal-pāṅkitaviṉatukuṟiyimuṭikkanērtal.maṭaṟkūṟṟoḻital-talivaṉatukuṟiyittīrkkappāṅkiuṭaṉpaṭṭamiyiṉtalivaṉnāṉmaṭalēṟuvaleṉṟukūṟiyapaṭimaṭalēṟāmalataṉiviṭuttal.kuṟinayappittal-pāṅkitalivaṉatukuṟiyimuṭittaṟkuttaliviyivirumpuvittal.nayattal-talivitalivaṉatukuṟiyimuṭikkuṭaṉpaṭal.kūṭṭal-pāṅkikuṟiyiṭattuttaliviyiuyttal.kūṭal-talivaṉtaliviyikkūṭutal.āyaṅkūṭṭal-pāṅkitaliviyiaḻittucceṉṟupāṅkiyariṭattilcērttal.vēṭṭal-talivaṉpāṅkiyiṉcoṟpaṭiviruntuvirumpal.
pāṅkimakiḻcci:
tōḻiyiṉatumakiḻcci.ituvariyumnāḷaḷavumāṟṟi
yiruntamiyittaliviyiyumtalivaṉiyumtōḻiviṉāvu
talālum,maṇamaṉivantacevilikkuttalivaṉukkumtalivikkumuḷḷaaṉpiyumuṟaviyumavarkaḷiṉvāḻkkiyiṉnalattiṉiyumkūṟutalālumviḷaṅkum.
pālikkuriyaperumpoḻutumciṟupoḻutum:
iḷavēṉiṟkālamum,mutuvēṉiṟkālamum,piṉpaṉik
kālamumākiyaperumpoḻutum,naṇpakalākiyaciṟupoḻutumpālittiṇikkuuriyaṉavām.

pirivuḻikkalaṅkal:
puṇarccikkaḷattiṉiṉṟutalivipirintapōtutalivaṉkalaṅkikkūṟutalpirivuḻikkalaṅkalākum.itumayaṅkiyaniliyilkūṟutalum,mayakkanteḷintapiṟakukūṟutalumeṉiraṇṭuvakiyiṉiyuṭiyatu.
pirivuḻikkalaṅkaliṉvirivu:
talivikāṇātatōraṇimikkaṇniṉṟatalimakaṉ,taliviāyakkūṭṭattilcērntavuṭaṉ,avarkuṟuṅ
kaṇṇiyumneṭuntōkiyumtaḷirumkoṇṭuvantuvaḻipaṭu
vārum,kuṟṟēvalceyvārumpallāṇṭukūṟuvārumāyccūḻntuniṟka,viṇmīṉnaṭuvaṇtaṇmatiyampōlavīṟṟiruk
kiṉṟatalivitaṉittuvantueṉṉikkūṭiyatueṉṉamāyamōeṉṟumayaṅkikkūṟutalum,taliviāyakkūṭṭattiṟcellum
pōtuuyirppāṅkimukattinōkkiccellutalaṟintu,avvuyirppāṅkiyittalivaṉtūtākappeṟṟuuyirvāḻvatākakkūṟutalum,taliviyiṉaḻakipparintukoṇṭāṭalumtaliviyittantapeṟṟōrkaḷittalivaṉvāḻttutalum,iraviltalivitantavēṭkiyāṉuṟakkampeṟātuiravukkālattinontukūṟutalumākiyaintumpirivuḻikkalaṅkaliṉviriyākum.
pirivumakiḻcci:
talimakaḷtaṉṉikkāṇāmaltāṉavaḷikkāṇpatōriṭattiṉkaṇniṉṟatalimakaṉpuṇarccikkaḷattiṉiṉṟupirintupōkiṉṟatalimakaḷatutaṉmiyikkaṇṭumakiḻtalpirivumakiḻcciyākum.itutaṉṉeñcoṭukūṟutal,pākaṉoṭukūṟutaleṉṉumiraṇṭupākupāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyatām.
pirivuṭaṉpaṭāmi:
talivaṉpirivukkuttaliviuṭaṉpaṭamaṟuttalpirivuṭaṉpaṭāmiyākum.
pirivaṟivuṟuttal:
talivaṉōtal,kāval,tūtu,tuṇi,poruḷākiyavikāraṇamākappiriyaviruttalittōḻitalivikkuuṇarttalpirivaṟivuṟuttalākum.

piṉpaṉikkālattiṉtaṉmi:
māci,paṅkuṉiākiyirumātaṅkaḷumpiṉpaṉikkāla
mākum.ikkālattuulavikkāṟṟuvīcum.palapaṟaviiṉamumkāṉakkōḻiyummakiḻum.kōṅkumilavamumpūkkum,pērīntupaṉipaḻukkum.paruttiveṭikkum.iviyēpiṉpaṉikkālattiṉtaṉmikaḷākum.
puṉaltarupuṇarcci:
itupāṅkicevilikkuaṟattoṭuniṟkummuṟikaḷuḷoṉṟu.niṉmakaḷumyāmumāṟṟilnīrāṭiṉōm.appōtuorucuḻivantutalimakaḷiamukkiiḻuttucceṉṟatu.avvaḻiyēvantatalivaṉavaḷieṭuttuvantāṉ.aṉṟumutalavaḷavaṉiyēeṇṇiuṭalmelikiṟāḷeṉṟutaliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkatticcevilittāykkuppāṅkiaṟivittal.
pukaḻtal:
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.talivaṉtali
viyiuvantukūṟutal.
pūttarupuṇarcci:
itucevilittāykkuttōḻitaliviyiṉkaḷavoḻuk
kattiveḷippaṭuttummuṟikaḷuḷoṉṟu.yāmcōliyilviḷiyāṭiyakālattuavvaḻiyēvantatalivaṉorupūvittaliviyiṭattukkoṭuttuaṇintukoḷkaveṉṟukūṟiṉāṉ.taliviyumataṉippeṟṟukkoṇṭāḷ.aṉṟumutalavaḷavaṉniṉivākavēirukkiṟāḷeṉṟupāṅkitaliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkatticcevilikkuppulappaṭuttutal.
peruntiṇi:
talimakaṉukkēuriyamaṭalēṟutalum,vayatumutirntatalimakaṉvayatiliḷiyavaḷākiyatalimakaḷiṭattuiṉpantuyttalum,vayatumutirntatalimakaḷvayatiliḷiyatalimakaṉiṭattuiṉpantuyttalum,talimakaṉumtalimakaḷumiḷaminīṅkimutumiyuṟṟakālattumaṟattiṉmēlmaṉamcellātukāmattiṉvayappaṭṭuniṟṟalum,aintiṇiyākiyottakāmattiṉmāṟupaṭṭuvarumnikaḻcciyumperuntiṇieṉappaṭum.
peruntiṇikkuriyameyppāṭu:
iṉpanalattiṉiveṟuttal,tuṉpattiṉkaṇṇēpulamputal;uruveḷippāṭukaṇṭuvaruntutal;kuṟṟamārāytal;paciyālvaruntiniṟṟal;pacaliparavutal;uṇavucuruṅkutal;uṇṇāmiyāluṭaliḷittal;uṟaṅkāmi;kaṉavinaṉaveṉamayaṅkutal;talivaṉkūṟṟippoyyākakkoḷḷutal;urittamāṟṟattimeyyēeṉṟukūṟutal;talivaṉkuṟippukaṇṭuaiyappaṭutal;talivaṉuṟaviṉarikkaṇṭaviṭattumakiḻtal;aṟattiṉiaḻittukkūṟumiṭattuneñcaḻintukūṟutal;evvuṭampāyiṉuntaṉṉōṭuoppumikoḷḷutal;talimakaṉoṭuoppākumeṉṟumaṟṟoṉṟikkaṇṭaviṭattumakiḻtal;talimakaṉpeyarkēṭṭumakiḻtal;maṉaṅkalaṅkutaleṉpaṉaperuntiṇikkuriyameyppāṭukaḷākum.
perumitam:
perumitamāṉatukalvicciṟappu,taṟukaṇmi,pukaḻmi,koṭittaṉmieṉṟucollappaṭṭanāṉkuvakikaḷālumuṇṭākum.perumitam-taṉṉipperiyaṉākamatittal.ivaṟṟuḷkalviyeṉpatu-tavammutalākiyavitti.taṟukaṇeṉpatu-añcattakkaṉakaṇṭaviṭattuañcāmi.pukaḻmiyeṉpatu-iṉpamumporuḷummikutiyākakkiṭippiṉumpaḻiyoṭuvaruvaṉaceyyāmi,koṭiyeṉpatu-uyirumuṭampum,uṟuppummutaliyellāpporuḷuṅkoṭuttal.
perumpoḻutiṉvaki:
kārkālam,kūtirkālam,muṉpaṉikkālam,piṉpaṉikkālam,iḷavēṉiṟkālam,mutuvēṉiṟkālameṉaperumpoḻutuaṟuvakippaṭum.
ivaṟṟuḷ,āvaṇittiṅkaḷum,puraṭṭācittiṅkaḷumkārkālamākum.aippacittiṅkaḷum,kārttikittiṅkaḷumkūtirkālamākum.mārkaḻittiṅkaḷum,tittiṅkaḷummuṉpaṉikkālamākum.mācittiṅkaḷumpaṅkuṉittiṅkaḷum,piṉpaṉikkālamākum;cittirittiṅkaḷum,vikācittiṅkaḷumiḷavēṉiṟkālamākum.āṉittiṅkaḷum,āṭittiṅkaḷummutuvēṉiṟ
kālamākum.
poruḷvayiṟpirivu:
talimakaṉporuḷīṭṭutalkāraṇamākappiriyumpirivuporuḷvayiṟpirivākum.ippirivuaracar,antaṇar,vaṇikar,vēḷāḷarākiyanālvarkkumurittām.ippirivuōrāṇṭukkālavelliyiṉiyuṭiyatu.
pōkkaṟivuṟuttal:(a)
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṭiyīṭṭiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.aimpattāṟāmnāḷtaṉṉūriviṭṭunīṅkuntalivietir
vantavarkaḷittalivaṉuṭaṉtāṉcellutalittōḻiyarkkumnaṟṟāykkumuṇarttaaṉupputalum,antaṇarkaḷtaliviyiṉcelavinaṟṟāykkukkūṟutalumām.
pōkkaṟivuṟuttal:(ā)
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.taliviyiuṭaṉaḻittuccellumāṟutalivaṉukkuttōḻikūṟutal.
pōkkal:
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.talivaṉuṭaṉtaliviyippāṅkicellaviṭal.
pōkkuṭaṉpaṭuttal:
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.uṭaṉkoṇṭucellutalaṉṟittalivikkuvēṟuaṭikkalamiṉmiyittalivaṉukkum,kaṟpiṉmēṉmiyittalivikkuṅkūṟiavviruvariyumuṭaṉpōkkiṟkuuṭaṉpaṭumāṟuceytal.
pōkkuṭaṉpaṭāmi:
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.tōḻitaliviyiuṭaṉaḻittuppōkumāṟukūṟiyatittalivaṉumtaliviyummaṟuttal.

muṉṉuṟavuṇartal:
itupāṅkimatiuṭaṉpāṭueṉṉumkaḷaviyalkiḷavittokikkuriyavakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattukkaṇtalivitalivaṉippuṇarntumīṇṭuvantupāṅkimuṉuṟṟa
vaḻippāṅki,pūviṉālum,cāntiṉālum,talivaṉuṭaṉkūṭiyakūṭṭattāltaliviyiṭattuuḷatākiyanaṟunāṟṟamum;kūṭṭat
tāṟpiṟantatōraḻakum;tōḻiyarkūṭṭattuṭaṉcērn
toḻukātutaṉṉittaṉiyākappēṇiyoḻukutalum;uṇṇumaḷaviṟkuṟitalum;tāṉceykiṉṟapūppaṟittalnīrāṭalmutaliyaviṉikaḷittōḻiaṟiyātavāṟumaṟittuttaṉiyēceytalum;etticiyilumceṉṟuviḷiyāṭupavaḷ.ippoḻutukuṟippiṭṭōriṭattiṟkēcellutalum;ōriṭattilēyēatikamākappaḻakutalumākiyēḻuvakimaṉanikaḻccikaḷittuṇiyākakkoṇṭutaliviyiaiyamuṟṟuārāyntumavaṟṟāliyanteḷintum,meyyiṉālumpoyyiṉālumpalvēṟuvakippaṭṭiruporuḷtarumcoṟkaḷālārāyntumpāṅkikūṭṭamuṇmiyiaṟintukoḷvāḷ.
mīṭci:-
ituvumkaḷavuveḷippāṭṭiṟkuriyakiḷavittokikaḷuḷoṉṟu.putalviyittēṭicceṉṟacevilimīṇṭuvarutalum,uṭaṉpōṉatalivaṉumtaliviyummīṇṭuvarutalumām.
mīṭciyiṉvaki:
teḷittal,makiḻcci,viṉātal,ceppaleṉṟanāṉkummīṭciyiṉvakikaḷākum.
mīṭciyiṉviri:-
talivineṭuntūramceṉṟamiyiccevilittāynaṟṟāykkuṇarttalum,aimpattunāṉkāmnāḷ,talivaṉmīṭciyiltaliviyatuūrittāmcārntamiyittalivikkukkūṟutalum,tamakkumuṉṉālcelpavariṭamtalivaṉoṭutāṉvaruvatippāṅkiyarkkuttalivikūṟiviṭuttalum,muṉceṉṟavartalivaṉtalivivarukiyippāṅkikkukkūṟutalum,acceytiyippāṅkiyarkēṭṭunaṟṟāykkukkūṟutalum,acceytiyikkēṭṭanaṟṟāytalivaṉtaliviyittaṉilliṟkuaḻittuvaruvāṉā?avaṉnaṟṟāyirukku
miṭattiṟkuaḻittuccelvāṉāeṉpativeṟi
yāṭṭāḷaṉiṭamviṉavutalumākiyāṟummīṭciyiṉvirikaḷākum.
meyppāṭu:
meyyiṉkaṇtōṉṟuvatumeyppāṭu.aḵtāvatu.veṇpaḷiṅkilcennūlkōttālataṉcemmipuṟattētōṉṟumāṟupōluḷḷaṅkarutiyaticcoṟṟaḷarvu,mey
viyarppu,kaṇṇīrnikaḻcci,meyvitirppu,meyvetumpal,meymmayirarumpalmutaliyakuṇaṅkaḷāṉveḷippaṭuttu
vatām.
meyppāṭṭiṉpeyar:
naki,aḻuki,iḷivaral,maruṭki,accam,perumitam,uvaki,vekuḷieṉpaṉameyppāṭṭiṉpeyarkaḷākum.ivaṟṟuḷ,nakieṉpatu-cirippu;atumuṟuvalittunakutalum,aḷavēcirittalum,perukaccirittalumeṉamūṉṟuvakip
paṭum.aḻukieṉpatu-tuyaram.tāṉētuyarappaṭutalum,piṟaratutuyaraṅkaṇṭavaḻituyarappaṭutalumeṉiruvakippaṭum.iḷivaraleṉpatu-iḻivu.maruṭkieṉpatu-viyappu.accameṉpatu-payam.perumitameṉpatu-vīram.vekuḷieṉpatu-kōpam.uvakiyeṉpatu-kāmammutaliyamakiḻcci.
meyppāṭṭiṟkuriyapiṟiṭaṅkaḷ:-
uṭimi,iṉpuṟutal,naṭunilimiyiṟniṟṟal,ellā
vuyirkkumaruḷceytal,taṉmipēṇutal,aṭakkam,nīkkavēṇṭiyavikaḷinīkkiyoḻukutal,aṉpu,aḷaviṟkuṟṟa
māyiṉumkuṇamāyiṉummikutal,piṟarivaruttal,cūḻcci,vāḻttutal,nāṇutal,tūṅkutal,uṟakkattiṉkaṇvāyccōrvupaṭal,ciṉattal,eṇṇutal,añcutal,cōmpalkarututal,ārāycci,kāriyavirivu,uyirppu,kiyāṟu,tuṉpam,maṟati,piṟarākkampoṟāmi,viyarttal,aiyam,oruvaṉinaṉkumatiyāmi,naṭukkamākiyamuppattiraṇṭum,nakimutalākuvakiīṟākakkūṟappaṭṭameyppāṭukaḷallātaviṭattumeyppāṭukaḷākavarum.
meyppāṭṭiṟkuoppumi:
ottapiṟappum,ottoḻukkamum,ottāṇmiyumottavayatum,ottaaḻakum,ottaaṉpum,ottaniṟiyum,ottaaruḷum,ottaaṟivum,ottacelvamumeṉṉumpattuvakiyumtalimakkaḷukkuirukkavēṇṭiyoppuppakutiyām.
meyppāṭṭiṉnuṭpam:
kaṇṇiṉālumceviyiṉālumnaṉṟākaaṟintu
koḷḷumaṟivuṭiyamakkaṭkallatumeyppāṭṭupporuḷkoḷḷutal,ārāytaṟkuarumiyuṭiyatākum.
meyyuṟupuṇarccinikaḻuṅkālam:
talimakaḷittalimakaṉtaṉiyiṭattukkāṇutalum,ivaḷippeṟavēṇṭumeṉṉumuḷḷanikaḻcciyum,iṭi
viṭātuniṉittalum,taṉeṇṇamkikūṭātuvarutaliṉuṇṇāmiyāluṭalmelitalum,taṉuḷḷattiluṇṭā
kiṉṟavaruttamikutiyippiṟarkkueṭutturittalum,nāṇattiṉelliyikkaṭattalum,tāṉkāṇukiṉṟaporuḷkaḷellāmtaliviyiṉuṟuppukaḷēpōlattōṟṟutalum,atuvē,pittātalum,atukāraṇamākamayakkamuṟutalum,kikūṭāta
viṭattuiṟantupaṭutalumākiyapattuavattikaḷumoruṅkēnikaḻumāyiṉtalimakaṉukkumeyyuṟupuṇarccikūṭutaṟkuriyatām.
mēvutal:
itutaṉmaṉivaritaliṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.talimakaṉyāṉtaliviyimaṇantukoṇṭamiyinumarkkukkūṟukeṉappāṅkiyiṭaṅkūṟutal.
varavuaṟivuṟuttal:
ituuṭaṉpōkkiṭiyīṭṭiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.taliviyiṉcuṟṟattārpiṉvarutalittalimakaḷkaṇṭutalivaṉukkuuṇarttal.
variviṭivittupporuḷvayiṟpiritaliṉvaki:
talivaṉtāṉtirumaṇattiiṭiyilēvittupporuḷkāraṇamākappirintupōvatittalivikkuaṟivikkumāṟutōḻiyiṭaṅkūṟutalākiyapirivaṟivuṟuttalum,talivaṉpirintucelvataṟkuttōḻiuṭaṉpaṭāmiyākiyapirivuṭaṉpaṭāmiyum,pāṅkiuṭaṉpaṭutaṟkuriyacoṟkaḷiccolliavaḷiuṭaṉpaṭacceytalākiyapirivuṭaṉpaṭuttalum,tirumaṇattiiṭiyilvittupporuḷkāraṇamākattalivaṉcellutaṟkuttōḻiuṭaṉpaṭutalākiyapirivuṭaṉpaṭutalum,talivaṉtirumaṇattiiṭiyēvittupporuḷkāraṇamākappirintapōtutalivimaṉaṅkalaṅkivaruntutalākiyapirivuḻikkalaṅkalum,tōḻitaliviyiiṭittukkūṟutalākiyavaṉpuṟiyum,talivaṉpiriviṉāluṇṭākiyapoṟuttaṟkariyatuṉpattippoṟuttukkoṇṭiruttalākiyavaṉpoṟiyum,talivaṉtirumpivarumvaḻiyiltaṉpirivāltaṉtalivikkuuṇṭākakkūṭiyāṟṟā
miyieṇṇimaṉaṅkalaṅkikkūṟutalākiyavarumvaḻikkalaṅka
lum,talivaṉtirumpivantapoḻututalivimakiḻtalākiyavantuḻimakiḻcciyumākiyoṉpatumvariviṭivittupporuḷvayiṟpiritaliṉvakikaḷākum.
variviṭivittupporuvayiṟpirital:
tirumaṇattiiṭiyilēvittuttirumaṇattiṟkuvēṇṭumporuḷkāraṇamākappiritalvariviṭivittupporuvayiṟpiritalākum.
varital:
aimpattāṟāmnāḷtalivaṉmīṇṭutalivimaṉikkaṇvārāviṭattu,taliviyiṉtamaretirkoṇṭupōyaḻittuvantapiṉulakavaḻakkappaṭipalavitamākaaruṅkalaṉmutaliyavēṇṭuvaṉakoṭuttuccāṉṟōrimuṉṉiṭṭumaṇaccaṭaṅkuṭaṉēvatuvimuṭittukkōṭalvaritalākum.
variviṟkuriyakiḷavittoki:
varivumalivu,aṟattoṭuniṟṟaleṉṟiraṇṭumvari
viṟkuriyakiḷavittokiyākum.
varivukaṭātal:
tōḻitalivaṉoṭuvarivukūṟiviṉātalvarivu
kaṭātalākum.
varivukaṭātaliṉvaki:
tōḻiyāṉavaḷtaliviyittalivaṉmaṇantukoḷḷumāṟuceyyeṇṇittalivaṉiṭampoyyāṉavaṟṟittāṉēpuṉintukūṟutalum,talivaṉkuṟikkaṇvarutalippoyyākavum,veḷippaṭiyākavuntōḻimaṟuttukkūṟutalum,talivaṉiṭam,nītaliviyimaṇantukoḷḷāmalkaḷaviṉkaṇoḻukutalniṉṉuṭiyanāṭumutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuēṟpuṭiyataṉṟueṉattōḻikūṟutalum,talivaṉukkumeyyāyiṉavaṟṟittōḻikūṟutalumākiyanāṉkumvarivukaṭātaliṉvakikaḷākum.
varivukaṭātaliṉviri:
taliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkatticcevilittāyaṟintutaṉṉiviṉaviyatākavum,tāṉataṟkumaṟittukkūṟiya
tākavum,tōḻitalivaṉiṭamkūṟutalum,ūriltaliviyittūṟṟumalarvirintateṉṟutalivaṉukkuaṟivuṟuttalum,kaḷavoḻukkattinaṟṟāyaṟintāḷeṉṟutalivaṟkukkūṟutalum,naṟṟāytaliviyiṉvēṟupāṭṭiṟkukkāraṇattiaṟiyaveṟiyāṭupavaṉiviṉaveṇṇiṉāḷeṉṟutali
makaṉukkuaccamuṟuttikkūṟutalum,taliviyippiṟarmaṇampēcivantataṉittalimakaṉukkuaṟivittalum,tōḻitalivaṉinōkkinīmaṇampēcieṅkaḷvaḷanakarkkuvantālemaretirkoṇṭuvaruvāreṉakkūṟutalum,maṇañceyvataṟkuriyanāḷiaṟivittalum,talimakaḷiṉaṟiviṉtiṟattittōḻitalimakaṉukkuaṟivuṟuttalum,tōḻitalivaṉiikkuṟikkaṇvarātuvēṟōrkuṟikkaṇvarukaveṉappaṇittalum,pakaṟkuṟivaruvāṉiiruṭkuṟikkaṇvarukaveṉakkūṟutalum,iruṭkuṟikkaṇvarupavaṉipakaṟ
kuṟikkaṇvarukaveṉakkūṟutalum,pakaṟkuṟiyilumiravuk
kuṟiyilumvarukaveṉakkūṟutalum,pakaṟkuṟiiravukkuṟiākiyiraṇṭukuṟikaḷilumvāraṟkeṉakkūṟutalum,tōḻitalivaṉiṭamniṉnāṭṭiṟkumūrukkum,kulapperumikkumkuṭimikkumpukaḻukkumvāymikkumnīceyyumnalviṉikkumtaliviyimaṇantukoḷḷātunaṭappatumuṟimiyaṉṟueṉakkūṟutalum,varumvaḻiyilvilaṅkāltōṉṟumaccattikkūṟutalum,talimakaḷatuāṟṟāmiyiavaḷimaṇañceytuāṟṟutalceyyattalimakaṉukkukkūṟutalum,kuṟiyiṭattunīvarutaṟkumavaḷvarutaṟkumkāvalmikutiyākavuḷḷateṉṟutalimakaṉukkukkūṟutalum,taliviyiṉkāmavēṭkimikkateṉṟukūṟutalum,tali
vikkukkaṉaviṉālvantatuṉpattittōḻitalimakaṉukkukkūṟutalum,taliviyiṉaḻakuaḻintamiyittalivaṉukkukkūṟutalumākiyirupatutuṟikaḷumvarivukaṭātaliṉviri
kaḷākum.
varivunikaḻuṅkālam:
taliviyiṉkaḷavoḻukkamveḷippaṭāmuṉṉum,veḷippaṭṭapiṉṉumvarivunikaḻum.
varitalvēṭki:
oṉpatāmnāḷiravuiruṭkuṟiiṭiyīṭupaṭṭataṉālpattāmnāḷtalivivaritalvēṭkiyāṟkūṟutalvaritalvēṭkiyākum.aḵtuaccam,uvarttal,āṟṟāmieṉamūvakippaṭum.accam-talivitalivaṉiaṭitaṟkuiṭi
yūṟāṉanikaḻccikaḷuṇṭākattaliviañcutal.uvarttal-tōḻitalivaṉiveṟuttukkūṟutal.āṟṟāmi-talivaṉvarāmiyittalivipoṟāmalvaruntutal.
varitalvēṭkiyiṉviri:
talimakaḷittōḻiniṉtuṉpattiṟkukkāraṇamyātueṉaviṉātalum,tamatukaḷavoḻukkatticceviliaṟintukoṇṭamiyittalivitōḻikkuaṟivittalum,talivaṉiravukkuṟikkaṇvarutaṟkuiṭiyūṟākiyanikaḻccikaḷnikaḻtalittōḻikkuttaliviterivittalum,namnalaṉuṇṭapātakariruppiṭattiaṟintuaṅkuccelvōmnīañcātēeṉṟupāṅkitalivikkukkūṟutalum,tōḻitalivaṉiṉtaṉmiyippaḻitturittalum,talivitalivaṉiṉiyalpippukaḻtalum,talivaṉkaṉavilkūṭiṉaṉāka,viḻittapiṉpupoyyāyppōṉatuṉpattippatiṉoṉṟāmnāḷtōḻiyiṭamtalivikūṟutalum,talivaṉuṭaṉkūṭappeṟāmiyāltaṉaḻakiḻanta
taṉittōḻikkuttalivikūṟutalum,taṉtuṉpattittalivaṟkuuṇarttalvēṇṭumeṉṟutōḻikkukkūṟutalum,yāṉkāmanōyāltuṉpamaṭitalittalivarkkunīceṉṟukūṟukaveṉattalivikūṟiyataṟkuttaliviyinōkkittōḻicollutalum.
uṟaviṉarkaḷumayalārumcollumalarikkarutiataṉāluṇṭāyapayattāltalimakaḷpāṅkiyiṭaṅkūṟutalum,iruṭkuṟikkaṇtalivaṉvantucellumvaḻiyikkarutiavvaḻiētattāluṇṭākiyaaccattāltalivikūṟuñcollum,kāmavēṭkimikkuḻikkaṭal,kāṉam,poḻil,vilaṅku,puḷivaṟṟinōkkittaliviiraṅkikkūṟutalum,talivitaṉṉiṭattuḷḷatuṉpattippiṟitoṉṟaṉmēliṭṭukkūṟutalum,talivaṉvarumvaḻiyivilakkeṉappāṅkiyoṭukūṟutalum,talivaṉvarumiruṭkuṟiyivilakkeṉattōḻiyōṭukūṟutalum,tāyveṟiyāṭutalkoṇṭāḷeṉṟutalivarkkukkūṟiiruṭkuṟivaruvativilakkeṉattalivitōḻikkukkūṟutalum,piṟarmaṇampēcivaruvatittalivarkkukkūṟivarivuvilakkeṉappāṅkikkuttalivikūṟutalum,periyōrivarivukūṟivaranamaretirkōṭalicceyyeṉṟutalivipāṅkiyoṭukūṟutalumākiyapatiṉeṭṭumvarivukaṭātaliṉvirikaḷākum.
variviṉilakkaṇam:
varivueṉṟucollappaṭuvatutalimakaṉtalimakaḷikkoṭuttaṟkuriyamuṟimiyiyuṭiyakuravarmutalāyiṉōrkoṭuppavum,koṭukkāviṭattumtirumaṇaccaṭaṅkoṭuporuntamaṇañceytukoḷvatām.
varivumalital:
varivutoṭaṅkinaṭakkummuyaṟcimikutalvarivumalita
lākum.itupāṅkivariviṉporuṭṭunaṭakkummuyaṟciyittalivikkutterivittalum,talivaṉcuṟṟattārmaṇaṅkūṟivantapōtutaliviyiṉcuṟṟattāretirntamiyittōḻitalivikkukkūṟutalum,tammuṭiyacuṟṟattārvarivuetirnta
taṉāltalivimakiḻtalum,maṇamnērntatamarēvalāyttalivimaṇattiṉporuṭṭutteyvattivaṇaṅkutalkaṇṭutalivaṉmakiḻtalumeṉanāṉkuvakippaṭum.
varivumalitaliṉviri:
peṇkoḷḷutaṟkuriyārpeṇkoṭuttaṟkuriyārkkukkoṭukkumporuḷikkātalaṉkoṭuttamiyittōḻitalimakaḷukkukkūṟutalum,kātalaṉkoṭuttaporuḷkaḷikkaṇṭu,makaṭku,maṇakkālameṉṟunaṟṟāymakiḻumuḷḷattunikaḻcciyittaliviniṉittalum,talivaṉcuṟṟattārmaṇampēcivantaviṭattuttaliviyiṉcuṟṟattāretirntamiyittōḻitalimakaṭkukkūṟutalum,icceytiyikkēḷviyuṟṟatalivimakiḻcciyaṭaṅkātuuḷḷattoṭukūṟutalum,talivaṉittōḻivāḻttutalum,talivimaṇamporuṭṭākatteyvattukkucciṟappucceytuvāḻttikkoṇṭuniṟkumniliyippāṅkitalimakaṟkukkāṭṭalum,taliviyiṉnilikaṇṭatalivaṉmakiḻtalumākiyēḻumvarivumalitaliṉvirikaḷākum.
variviṭivittupporuḷvayiṟpiritaliṉviri:-
talivaṉtaṉtirumaṇattiṟkuvēṇṭiyaporuḷkāraṇa
mākappiriyappōvatittalivikkukkūṟumāṟutōḻiyiṭaṅ
kūṟutalum,tōḻi,niṉpirivinīyēavaṭkukkūṟukaveṉakkūṟutalum,talivaṉkālaṅkaṭakkāmalvirintuvaruvēṉeṉṟupāṅkiyoṭukūṟinīṅkutalum,pāṅkitalivaṉpirintuceṉṟatittalivikkukkūṟutalum,talivaṉcelaviaṟintatalimakaḷvaruntutalum,ivvāṟunīvaruntuvatutakātueṉṟutōḻitaliviyiiṭittukkūṟutalum,iṭittukkūṟiyapāṅkiyittalivimaṉattuḷnontukūṟutalum,pāṅkiiṭittukkūṟiyataṉiūriṉmēlvittukkūṟutalum,talivarmīṇṭuvaruvareṉattōḻiāṟṟuvittalum,talivaṉpirintuceṉṟakālattu,kārkālattiṟkumuṉṉēvaruvēṉeṉṟukuṟippālkūṟippōyiṉāṉ.ākiyālapparuvaṅkaṇṭutalimakaḷpulamputalum,avvāṟuvaruntiyataliviyiṉtuṉpamnīṅkumvaṇṇamitukārkālamēkamaṉṟu,kālamallākkālattiltōṉṟiyamēkameṉṟutōḻikūṟutalum,talimakaḷtōḻikūṟiyataṉimaṟuttukkūṟutalum,talivikārkālamvantateṉṟukūṟiyakūṟṟikkēṭṭapāṅki,talivaṉtāṉvarukiṉṟaceytiyiaṟivittaṟkuviṭuppippoḻutuimmēkamvantuaṭintateṉṟukūṟutalum,atikkēṭṭatalimakaḷāṟṟiyiruttalum,poruḷtiraṭṭacceṉṟatalimakaṉtaṉceyalmuṭintapiṟakuavviṭattuttaliviyiniṉittuvaruntutalum,ceyalmuṭintutirumpivaruṅkāltērivirintuceluttukaveṉattalivaṉpākaṉoṭukūṟutalum,talivaṉvaruṅkālattumēkattinōkkikkūṟutalum,tōḻivalampuriyōciyikkēṭṭuttalivaṉvaravittalimakaḷukkukkūṟutalum,valampuriyittalivivāḻttutalum,talivaṉpirintuceṉṟakālattueṅkaḷiniṉittīrāeṉattōḻiviṉātalum,ataṟkuttalivaṉuṅkaḷimaṟantālallavāniṉippataṟkueṉakkūṟutalum,taliviyiāṟṟuvittiruntaarumiyittalimakaṉukkuttōḻikūṟutalumākiyirupattoṉṟumvariviṭittupporuvayiṟpiritaliṉviriyākum.
varumvaḻikkalaṅkal:
viṉimuṟṟiyatalivaṉmīṇṭuvarumvaḻiyiltaliviyiṉniliyieṇṇivaruntutalvarumvaḻikkalaṅkalākum.
vaṉpuṟi:(a)
talivaṉpirintaviṭattuttalivivaruntuvāḷ.avvāṟuvaruntuntaliviyittōḻiiṭittukkūṟutalvaṉpuṟiyākum.
vaṉpuṟi-(ā)
taliviaiyuṟṟavaḻittalivaṉiyamnīṅkavaṟpuṟuttikkūṟutalvaṉpuṟiyākum.vaṉpu-valimi.uṟi-uṟuttal.ituaiyantīrttal,pirivuṟuttaleṉiruvakippaṭum.aiyantīrtta
lāvatutalivikkuṇṭāṉaiyattittalivaṉnīkkutal.piri
vuṟuttalāvatutalivaṉtaṉpirivittalivikkuaṟivittal.
vaṉpoṟi:
talivaṉpirivittalivivalitiṟpoṟuttukkoḷḷutalvaṉpoṟiyākum.
vāyilnērtal:
pāṇaṉmutaliyavarkaḷiṉkūṟṟukkiṇaṅkattalimakaḷvāyiliēṟṟukkoḷḷutalvāyilnērtalākum.
vāyilnērvittal:
pāṇaṉ,tōḻimutaliyōrtalimakaḷivāyiliēṟṟukkoḷḷumāṟuceytalvāyilnērvittalākum.
vāyilmaṟuttal:
pāṇaṉmutaliyavarkaḷittalimakaṉvāyilākaaṉuppattalivimaṟuttalvāyilmaṟuttalākum.
vāyilvēṇṭal:
parattiyiṟpirintatalimakaṉtōḻiyittaṉakkuvāyi
lākumāṟuvēṇṭutalvāyilvēṇṭalākum.
vilakkal:
iḵtuuṭaṉpōkkiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.talivaṉtaliviyiṉtaḷarccikaṇṭuavaḷuṭaṉōriṭattiltaṅkiya
pōtukaṇṭōravarkaḷmītuaṉpukoṇṭuavarkaḷcelavivilakkittaṅkaḷōṭutaṅkiccellumāṟukūṟutal.
viṉātal:(1)
itutaṉmaṉivaritaliṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.taliviyiṉnaṟṟāynamilliṉkaṇnamputalvikkuttirumaṇamnaṭattumāṟutalimakaṉnaṟṟāyikkēṭpōmāeṉṟuceviliyiviṉātal.
viṉātal:(2)
itumīṭciyiṉvakikaḷuḷoṉṟu.
uṭaṉpōkkucceṉṟataṉmakaḷkātalaṉuṭaṉvarukiṉṟāḷeṉpataṉiaṟintanaṟṟāynamputalviyinammaṉikkēkoṇṭuvaruvāṉō?taṉneṭunakarkkēkoṇṭucelvāṉō?eṉṟuveṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉiviṉāvutal.
vekuḷi:
itueṇvakimeyppāṭukaḷuḷoṉṟu.uṟuppukaḷmutaliyavaṟṟiaṟuttal,taṉkīḻvāḻvōrittuṉpuṟuttal,vitalaṭittal,kolikkuoruppaṭutalākiyanāṉkuvakiyāṉaveṟukkattakkaceyalkaḷālvekuḷitōṉṟum.

ellinīrñālatticiviḷaṅkat-tolli
iravāmalīntiṟivarpōlnīyum
karavāmalīkikaṭaṉ.
iyalmoḻivāḻttu:-ā
mayakkamillātaperiyapukaḻiṉiyumkutiripūṭṭiyatēriṉiyumuṭiyaaracaṉatutaṉmiyiṉieṭuttukkūṟiṉumiyalmoḻivāḻttueṉṉumtuṟiyām.

kaḻiyāmēmaṉṉarkataṅkāṟṟumvēlāṉ
oḻiyāmēōmpumulaku.
iravuttaliccēṟal:

piṇiyāramārpampiṇiyat-tuṇiyāyk
kaḻikāmamuyppakkaṉiyiruṭkaṇcelkēṉ
vaḻikāṇamiṉṉukavāṉ.
iravunīṭuparuvaral:
pakaṟpoḻutiveṟuttuttaṉimiyōṭuuṟintataliviirāppoḻutilkāmattālmikavumtuṉpamaṭintēṉeṉṟuneñcamnekiḻntucolliyatuiravunīṭuparuvaraleṉṉumtuṟiyām.
(e.ṭu)
peṇmēlnalivupiḻiyeṉṉāypētuṟīi
viṇmēliyaṅkummativilakki-maṇmēl
niṉakkēceypakievaṉkol
eṉakkēneṭiyivāḻiyariravē.
iruvakippaṭṭapiḷḷinili:
iruvakippaṭṭapiḷḷiniliyāvatu,taṉmēlvarukiṉṟakoṭippaṭiyietirttuniṟṟalum,taṉṉuṭiyavāḷvaṉmiyālpakivariyumvīḻttittāṉumvīḻntupaṭuvatumākiyiraṇṭupākupāṭukaḷiyuṭiyapōril,maṟavarkaḷtāmēceyyumañcāmiyām.
(e.ṭu)
ēṟṟeṟintārtārtāṅkivelvarukeṉṟēviṉāṉ
kūṟṟiṉuntāyēkoṭiyaḷē-pōrkkaḷiṟu
kāṇāviḷamiyārkaṇṭivaṉōniṉṟilēṉ
māṇāruḷyārpiḻippārmaṟṟu
iḵtuvarutārtāṅkal.
āṭumpoḻutiṉaṟukayiṟṟuppāvipōl

ituvāḷvāyttukkaviḻtal.
